# Crewluve Web Slice 1 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build crewluve.crewlu.net slice 1. The visitor signs in with the pilot's CrewLu Cloud account and sees Crewluve's "Where's {Name}?" sentence card and home countdown, worked out from CrewLu Cloud data.

**Architecture:** A Vite + TypeScript + Preact single-page app.

- **Pure modules**, each tested in isolation:
  - `src/time` — wall-clock time in any time zone
  - `src/engine` — database rows → schedule → legs → status → countdown targets → one `Snapshot`
  - `src/copy` — formatting, airline branding, Crewluve's sentences, icons and colors
- **`src/data`** — the read-only Supabase fetch, the local cache, the demo schedule and the airport list.
- **`src/ui`** — Preact components that render a `Snapshot`.

**Tech Stack:**
- Vite 5.4.21, TypeScript 5.9.3, Preact 10.29.8 (+ @preact/preset-vite 2.10.6), @supabase/supabase-js 2.117.3
- Tests: Vitest 3.2.7 with happy-dom 20.14.5 and @testing-library/preact 3.2.4 (upgraded from Vitest 2.1.9 and happy-dom 15.11.7 during Batch A, for security advisories)
- Build-time only, installed on demand and never saved: geo-tz 8.1.9 (airport time zones) and @phosphor-icons/core 2.1.1 (icon paths)
- Python 3 + Pillow, already on this Mac, for the favicons

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-08-crewluve-web-slice1-design.md`. Read it first. Section numbers below (§) refer to it.

---

## Conventions for every task

- **Working directory.** Run every command from the project root, `~/Dev/Crewluve Website`. Quote it in shells; it has a space.
- **Test anchor.** Engine and copy tests use one fixed moment:
  - `A = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 9, 30)`, which is Fri 9 Oct 2026, 5:30am EDT. That is the report time of the demo pilot's main trip.
  - Minute offsets are relative to `A`; `at(m) = A + m * 60_000`.
  - Formatting tests always pass an explicit time zone (`America/New_York`) and a 12/24-hour flag, so they pass on any machine (CI runs in UTC).
- **UI tests.** Test files that render Preact start with the docblock `// @vitest-environment happy-dom` and call `afterEach(cleanup)`.
- **Commits.** End every commit message with:

  ```
  Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
  ```

  - The user approved committing during execution when they asked for git and this plan on 2026-10-08.
  - **Never push, create the GitHub repo, or touch DNS without asking the user first** (Task 33).
- **Read-only.** Never run tests against the real CrewLu Cloud. Nothing in this plan writes to the database.
- **Gates before calling any task done:** `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npx vitest run`, both clean. `npm run build` must also pass from Task 1 onward.

## Execution notes (read before your batch)

Tasks 1–8 are done on branch `slice1` and passed both reviews. Review fixes changed some of their code from the blocks printed in those tasks. **The repo is the source of truth for files that already exist.** Later tasks depend on these changes.

**From Batch A (Tasks 1–4):**

- **Test tooling.** Vitest 3.2.7 and happy-dom 20.14.5. The UI test patterns in this plan work unchanged.
- **TypeScript library.** `tsconfig.json` uses `lib: ["ES2020", "DOM", "DOM.Iterable"]` (matching the ES2020 build target) and `noEmit`.
  - Don't use ES2021+ library APIs: `String#replaceAll`, `Array#at`, `Object.hasOwn`, `Promise.any`.
  - Syntax is fine; esbuild lowers it.
  - The plan's remaining code already follows this.
- **`src/time/zoned.ts`** adds two exports:
  - `isKnownZone(tz)`: a memoized check that this runtime's `Intl` can format times in the zone.
  - `viewerTimeZone(resolve?)`: returns "UTC" when the browser reports no usable zone. App code calls it with no arguments.
- **`src/data/airports.ts`:**
  - Codes are trimmed and uppercased. Blank-code rows are skipped.
  - `city()` falls back to the code when the city name is blank.
  - `tz()` returns null for unknown airports and for zones the runtime rejects. Callers fall back to the viewer's zone.
  - `get(code).tz` is the raw value from the file. Do time math with `tz()`.
- **`scripts/build-airports.mjs`:**
  - Uses `geo-tz/all`.
  - Writes three new zone names as their older aliases (`OLDER_NAMES`) and two as same-rules stand-ins (`SAME_RULES_TODAY`).
  - `AIRPORTS_SOURCE` overrides the source path.
- **`vite.config.ts`.** The CSP adds `object-src 'none'`. The build throws if `index.html` loses `<!--csp-->`. `tests/viteConfig.test.ts` tests the plugin.
- **`package.json`** has `"engines": { "node": ">=22" }`.

**From Batch B (Tasks 5–8):**

- **Demo delay timing.** `src/data/demo.ts` exports `DELAY_KNOWN_AT = 1300`, and `demoRaw(variant, anchor, delayKnown = true)` has a third parameter.
  - `demoRawForScene` passes `offset >= DELAY_KNOWN_AT`, so scenes before `delayed` show the ANC→SDF flight on time. Before this, the default `flying` scene showed a red, late homecoming a day early.
  - `demoRaw(variant, anchor)` still includes the 40-minute delay, so engine tests that use the full timeline are unchanged.
- **`flightPhrase`** returns '' unless the flight number is all digits. Blank airline codes count as none ("Commercial").
- **`RawBuilder.build()`** copies rows with JSON instead of `structuredClone`, so demo mode runs on Safari 14.
- **Invisible characters.** `format.ts` writes the narrow no-break space (U+202F) as the escape `\u202F`.
  - Code and tests in this plan use the escape too.
  - Copy plan blocks with a script rather than retyping them. Editors and tools can silently turn special characters into plain spaces.

**Full suite after Batch B:** 122 tests in 9 files. Steps that run a single test file are unaffected. A full-suite run just has more tests than a task's own count.

```ts
import { defineConfig } from 'vite'
import preact from '@preact/preset-vite'

// Production Content-Security-Policy (spec §9). Injected only into the built HTML, because the
// Vite dev server needs inline scripts and a websocket for hot reload.
const CSP = [
  "default-src 'self'",
  "script-src 'self'",
  "style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'",
  "img-src 'self' data:",
  "font-src 'self'",
  "connect-src 'self' https://corxvurxpnrzekbxdgye.supabase.co",
  "base-uri 'self'",
  "form-action 'self'",
].join('; ')

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [
    preact(),
    {
      name: 'crewluve-csp',
      apply: 'build',
      transformIndexHtml: (html) =>
        html.replace('<!--csp-->', `<meta http-equiv="Content-Security-Policy" content="${CSP}" />`),
    },
  ],
  server: { port: 5180, strictPort: true },
  preview: { port: 5181 },
  build: { target: 'es2020' },
})
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { calendarDay, prefers24Hour, zoneAbbr, zonedParts } from '../../src/time/zoned'

const T = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 3, 30) // 2026-10-09T03:30Z

describe('zonedParts', () => {
  it('reads wall-clock parts in the zone', () => {
    expect(zonedParts(T, 'America/New_York')).toEqual({ year: 2026, month: 10, day: 8, hour: 23, minute: 30, weekday: 4 })
    expect(zonedParts(T, 'Asia/Hong_Kong')).toEqual({ year: 2026, month: 10, day: 9, hour: 11, minute: 30, weekday: 5 })
  })

  it('uses hour 0 at midnight', () => {
    expect(zonedParts(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 4, 0), 'America/New_York').hour).toBe(0)
  })
})

describe('calendarDay', () => {
  it('changes across a local midnight', () => {
    expect(calendarDay(T, 'Asia/Hong_Kong') - calendarDay(T, 'America/New_York')).toBe(1)
  })
})

describe('zoneAbbr', () => {
  it('prints US abbreviations and GMT offsets elsewhere, like iOS', () => {
    expect(zoneAbbr(T, 'America/New_York')).toBe('EDT')
    expect(zoneAbbr(Date.UTC(2026, 0, 9), 'America/New_York')).toBe('EST')
    expect(zoneAbbr(T, 'Pacific/Honolulu')).toBe('HST')
    expect(zoneAbbr(T, 'Europe/Berlin')).toBe('GMT+2')
  })
})

describe('prefers24Hour', () => {
  it('follows the locale', () => {
    expect(prefers24Hour('en-US')).toBe(false)
    expect(prefers24Hour('en-GB')).toBe(true)
  })
})
```

- [x] **Step 3: Write `src/time/zoned.ts`**

```ts
export const MINUTE = 60_000
export const HOUR = 3_600_000
export const DAY = 86_400_000

export const WEEKDAYS = ['Sunday', 'Monday', 'Tuesday', 'Wednesday', 'Thursday', 'Friday', 'Saturday'] as const
export const MONTHS = [
  'January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June',
  'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December',
] as const

/** Calendar parts of an instant as seen in an IANA time zone. `weekday` is 0 = Sunday. */
export interface ZonedParts {
  year: number
  month: number
  day: number
  hour: number
  minute: number
  weekday: number
}

const SHORT_WEEKDAYS = ['Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat']
const formatters = new Map<string, Intl.DateTimeFormat>()

function formatterFor(timeZone: string): Intl.DateTimeFormat {
  let f = formatters.get(timeZone)
  if (!f) {
    f = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {
      timeZone,
      year: 'numeric',
      month: 'numeric',
      day: 'numeric',
      hour: 'numeric',
      minute: 'numeric',
      hourCycle: 'h23',
      weekday: 'short',
    })
    formatters.set(timeZone, f)
  }
  return f
}

export function zonedParts(ms: number, timeZone: string): ZonedParts {
  const parts = formatterFor(timeZone).formatToParts(ms)
  const get = (type: string) => parts.find((p) => p.type === type)?.value ?? ''
  return {
    year: Number(get('year')),
    month: Number(get('month')),
    day: Number(get('day')),
    hour: Number(get('hour')) % 24,
    minute: Number(get('minute')),
    weekday: SHORT_WEEKDAYS.indexOf(get('weekday')),
  }
}

/** Days since the epoch for the calendar date of `ms` in `timeZone` (for "today"/"tomorrow" math). */
export function calendarDay(ms: number, timeZone: string): number {
  const p = zonedParts(ms, timeZone)
  return Date.UTC(p.year, p.month - 1, p.day) / DAY
}

/** Zone abbreviation the way iOS prints it: "EDT", "HST", or "GMT+2" outside the US. */
export function zoneAbbr(ms: number, timeZone: string): string {
  const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { timeZone, timeZoneName: 'short' }).formatToParts(ms)
  return parts.find((p) => p.type === 'timeZoneName')?.value ?? ''
}

export function viewerTimeZone(): string {
  return Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone
}

/** True when the locale uses a 24-hour clock (Crewluve follows the device's 12/24-hour setting). */
export function prefers24Hour(locale?: string): boolean {
  const options = new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, { hour: 'numeric' }).resolvedOptions()
  if (options.hourCycle) return options.hourCycle === 'h23' || options.hourCycle === 'h24'
  return options.hour12 === false
}
```

### Task 3: Icon paths

SF Symbols can't ship on a website (§4.1), so each symbol becomes a Phosphor "fill" icon. Every one of the icons below is a single `<path>` in a 256×256 box; this was checked on 2026-10-08. The generator copies the path data into a committed TypeScript file, so nothing renders raw SVG markup.

**Files:**
- Create: `scripts/build-icons.mjs`, `src/icons/iconPaths.ts` (generated), `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md`
- Test: `tests/icons/iconPaths.test.ts`

- [x] **Step 3: Write `scripts/build-icons.mjs`**

```js
// Copies the Phosphor "fill" path data this site uses into src/icons/iconPaths.ts.
// Run with `npm run icons` (installs @phosphor-icons/core on demand, never saved to package.json).
import { mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { dirname, resolve } from 'node:path'
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url'

const root = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..')
const fillDir = resolve(root, 'node_modules/@phosphor-icons/core/assets/fill')
const outFile = resolve(root, 'src/icons/iconPaths.ts')

// Component name -> Phosphor file stem. The first block maps the SF Symbols Crewluve uses on these
// cards (spec §4.1); the second maps AirlineBranding's airline symbols (spec §6.10).
const ICONS = {
  House: 'house', Airplane: 'airplane', Car: 'car', ArrowsClockwise: 'arrows-clockwise', Bed: 'bed',
  Buildings: 'buildings', ClockCountdown: 'clock-countdown', Lightning: 'lightning', BookOpen: 'book-open',
  MapPin: 'map-pin', Circle: 'circle', Package: 'package', AirplaneTakeoff: 'airplane-takeoff',
  AirplaneLanding: 'airplane-landing', Cloud: 'cloud', ArrowCircleUp: 'arrow-circle-up', Warning: 'warning',
  ArrowClockwise: 'arrow-clockwise', SignOut: 'sign-out', Heart: 'heart',

  Triangle: 'triangle', Star: 'star', GlobeHemisphereWest: 'globe-hemisphere-west', AirplaneTilt: 'airplane-tilt',
  Mountains: 'mountains', CurrencyCircleDollar: 'currency-circle-dollar', Leaf: 'leaf', Sun: 'sun', Wind: 'wind',
  Bird: 'bird', Crown: 'crown', Rabbit: 'rabbit', Sparkle: 'sparkle', CircleHalf: 'circle-half',
  GlobeHemisphereEast: 'globe-hemisphere-east', Diamond: 'diamond', Plus: 'plus', CaretUpDown: 'caret-up-down',
  Sailboat: 'sailboat', PawPrint: 'paw-print', NavigationArrow: 'navigation-arrow',
}

const lines = []
for (const [name, stem] of Object.entries(ICONS)) {
  const svg = readFileSync(resolve(fillDir, `${stem}-fill.svg`), 'utf8')
  const paths = [...svg.matchAll(/<path[^>]*\sd="([^"]+)"/g)].map((m) => m[1])
  if (paths.length === 0 || /<(rect|circle|ellipse|line|polyline|polygon)\b/.test(svg)) {
    throw new Error(`${stem}-fill.svg is not plain path data`)
  }
  lines.push(`  ${name}: ${JSON.stringify(paths)},`)
}

mkdirSync(dirname(outFile), { recursive: true })
writeFileSync(
  outFile,
  `// GENERATED by scripts/build-icons.mjs from @phosphor-icons/core 2.1.1 (MIT), "fill" weight, 256×256.
// Do not edit by hand; run \`npm run icons\`.
export const ICON_PATHS = {
${lines.join('\n')}
} as const

export type IconName = keyof typeof ICON_PATHS
`,
)
console.log(`wrote ${lines.length} icons to ${outFile}`)
```

- [x] **Step 3: Write `src/data/airports.ts`**

```ts
/** One airport in public/data/airports.json. */
export type AirportTuple = [iata: string, city: string, country: string, lat: number, lng: number, tz: string]

export interface Airport {
  iata: string
  city: string
  country: string
  lat: number
  lng: number
  tz: string
}

export interface AirportIndex {
  get(code: string | null | undefined): Airport | undefined
  /** City name, falling back to the code itself; null only when there is no code. */
  city(code: string | null | undefined): string | null
  /** IANA time zone, or null for unknown airports. */
  tz(code: string | null | undefined): string | null
}

export function buildAirportIndex(rows: AirportTuple[]): AirportIndex {
  const map = new Map<string, Airport>()
  for (const [iata, city, country, lat, lng, tz] of rows) {
    const code = iata.toUpperCase()
    map.set(code, { iata: code, city, country, lat, lng, tz })
  }
  const clean = (code: string | null | undefined) => (code ? code.trim().toUpperCase() : '')
  const get = (code: string | null | undefined) => map.get(clean(code))
  return {
    get,
    city: (code) => (clean(code) ? get(code)?.city ?? clean(code) : null),
    tz: (code) => get(code)?.tz ?? null,
  }
}

export async function loadAirports(url = '/data/airports.json'): Promise<AirportIndex> {
  const res = await fetch(url)
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`airports.json ${res.status}`)
  return buildAirportIndex((await res.json()) as AirportTuple[])
}
```

- [x] **Step 4: Write `scripts/build-airports.mjs`**

```js
// Builds public/data/airports.json as [iata, city, country, lat, lng, tz] tuples.
// Source: the Flight Globe's merged list (Crewlu's own airport tables win over OurAirports there).
// Time zones: geo-tz, installed on demand by `npm run airports` and never saved as a dependency.
import { mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { createRequire } from 'node:module'
import { dirname, resolve } from 'node:path'
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url'

const require = createRequire(import.meta.url)
const { find } = require('geo-tz')

const root = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..')
const source = process.env.AIRPORTS_SOURCE ?? '~/Dev/crewlu-globe/public/data/airports.json'
const outFile = resolve(root, 'public/data/airports.json')

const seen = new Set()
const out = []
for (const r of JSON.parse(readFileSync(source, 'utf8'))) {
  const iata = String(r.iata ?? '').trim().toUpperCase()
  if (!/^[A-Z0-9]{3}$/.test(iata) || seen.has(iata)) continue
  if (typeof r.lat !== 'number' || typeof r.lng !== 'number') continue
  const tz = find(r.lat, r.lng)[0]
  if (!tz) continue
  seen.add(iata)
  const round = (n) => Math.round(n * 1e4) / 1e4
  out.push([iata, String(r.city ?? iata), String(r.country ?? ''), round(r.lat), round(r.lng), tz])
}
out.sort((a, b) => (a[0] < b[0] ? -1 : 1))
mkdirSync(dirname(outFile), { recursive: true })
writeFileSync(outFile, JSON.stringify(out))
console.log(`wrote ${out.length} airports to ${outFile}`)
```

```ts
  .trip('t1', { days: 1, dutyPeriods: [{ start: 0, end: 120, flights: [{ dep: 'SDF', arr: 'RFD', out: 30, in: 100 }] }] })
  .commute('c1', 'LEX', 'SDF', 'toWork', -60, -10, 't1')

})

describe('RawBuilder', () => {

    expect(raw.trips[0].start_date).toBe('2026-10-09T09:30:00.000Z')
```

```ts
// Row shapes of the CrewLu Cloud tables this site reads (spec §6.1). Timestamps are ISO-8601 UTC strings.

export interface PilotInfoRow {
  first_name: string | null
  base_raw_value: string | null
  home_airport_code: string | null
  is_active: boolean | null
  updated_at: string
}

export interface TripRow {
  id: string
  start_date: string | null
  end_date: string | null
  days: number | null
  base: string | null
  updated_at: string
}

export interface DutyPeriodRow {
  id: string
  trip_id: string | null
  day: number | null
  start_time: string | null
  end_time: string | null
  updated_at: string
}

export interface FlightRow {
  id: string
  trip_id: string | null
  duty_period_id: string | null
  flight_number: string | null
  airline_code: string | null
  departure: string | null
  arrival: string | null
  scheduled_block_out_time: string | null
  scheduled_block_in_time: string | null
  block_out_time: string | null
  block_in_time: string | null
  is_dh: boolean | null
  is_commercial_deadhead: boolean | null
  is_ground_transport: boolean | null
  hidden_from_timeline: boolean | null
  updated_at: string
}

export interface CommuteRow {
  id: string
  from_airport: string | null
  to_airport: string | null
  direction: string | null
  departure_time_zulu: string | null
  arrival_time_zulu: string | null
  source_trip_id: string | null
  hidden_from_timeline: boolean | null
  updated_at: string
}

export interface JumpseatRow {
  id: string
  source_trip_id: string | null
  flight_number: string | null
  airline_code: string | null
  origin: string | null
  destination: string | null
  departure_time_zulu: string | null
  arrival_time_zulu: string | null
  status_raw_value: string | null
  is_commercial_deadhead: boolean | null
  hidden_from_timeline: boolean | null
  updated_at: string
}

export interface TrainingEventRow {
  id: string
  event_type: string | null
  start_time_utc: string | null
  end_time_utc: string | null
  station: string | null
  hidden_from_timeline: boolean | null
  updated_at: string
}

export interface ManualEventRow {
  id: string
  parent_event_id: string | null
  category_id: string | null
  title: string | null
  start_time_utc: string | null
  end_time_utc: string | null
  location: string | null
  hidden_from_timeline: boolean | null
  updated_at: string
}

export interface EventCategoryRow {
  id: string
  name: string | null
}

/** The rows of one fetch, per table. */
export interface ScheduleRows {
  pilotInfos: PilotInfoRow[]
  trips: TripRow[]
  dutyPeriods: DutyPeriodRow[]
  flights: FlightRow[]
  commutes: CommuteRow[]
  jumpseats: JumpseatRow[]
  trainingEvents: TrainingEventRow[]
  manualEvents: ManualEventRow[]
  eventCategories: EventCategoryRow[]
}

export interface RawSchedule extends ScheduleRows {
  /** When the site fetched these rows (epoch ms). */
  fetchedAt: number
  /** Newest updated_at among the rows: the last time the pilot's phone sent a change. */
  newestUpdatedAt: number | null
}

/** PostgREST column lists; they must match the interfaces above (tests/data/rows.test.ts checks). */
export const COLUMNS = {
  pilot_infos: 'first_name,base_raw_value,home_airport_code,is_active,updated_at',
  trips: 'id,start_date,end_date,days,base,updated_at',
  duty_periods: 'id,trip_id,day,start_time,end_time,updated_at',
  flights:
    'id,trip_id,duty_period_id,flight_number,airline_code,departure,arrival,scheduled_block_out_time,scheduled_block_in_time,block_out_time,block_in_time,is_dh,is_commercial_deadhead,is_ground_transport,hidden_from_timeline,updated_at',
  commutes:
    'id,from_airport,to_airport,direction,departure_time_zulu,arrival_time_zulu,source_trip_id,hidden_from_timeline,updated_at',
  jumpseats:
    'id,source_trip_id,flight_number,airline_code,origin,destination,departure_time_zulu,arrival_time_zulu,status_raw_value,is_commercial_deadhead,hidden_from_timeline,updated_at',
  training_events: 'id,event_type,start_time_utc,end_time_utc,station,hidden_from_timeline,updated_at',
  manual_events:
    'id,parent_event_id,category_id,title,start_time_utc,end_time_utc,location,hidden_from_timeline,updated_at',
  event_categories: 'id,name',
} as const

export function newestUpdatedAt(rows: ScheduleRows): number | null {
  const tables: { updated_at: string }[][] = [
    rows.pilotInfos, rows.trips, rows.dutyPeriods, rows.flights,
    rows.commutes, rows.jumpseats, rows.trainingEvents, rows.manualEvents,
  ]
  let newest: number | null = null
  for (const table of tables) {
    for (const row of table) {
      const t = Date.parse(row.updated_at)
      if (Number.isFinite(t) && (newest == null || t > newest)) newest = t
    }
  }
  return newest
}
```

- [x] **Step 4: Write `src/data/rowBuilder.ts`**

```ts
import { newestUpdatedAt, type PilotInfoRow, type RawSchedule, type ScheduleRows } from './rows'

const MIN = 60_000

export interface FlightSpec {
  dep: string
  arr: string
  /** Scheduled block out/in, in minutes from the anchor. */
  out: number
  in: number
  /** Actual/ETD times in minutes; omitted means "same as scheduled", which is how Crewlu stores on-time flights. */
  actualOut?: number
  actualIn?: number
  /** Default '1000' (a company flight). */
  number?: string | null
  /** Default null (company flight). */
  airline?: string | null
  dh?: boolean
  commercialDh?: boolean
  ground?: boolean
  hidden?: boolean
}

export interface DutySpec {
  start: number
  end: number
  flights: FlightSpec[]
}

export interface TripSpec {
  days?: number
  base?: string
  dutyPeriods: DutySpec[]
}

/** Builds CrewLu Cloud rows from minute offsets around an anchor instant (tests and the demo). */
export class RawBuilder {
  private readonly rows: ScheduleRows = {
    pilotInfos: [], trips: [], dutyPeriods: [], flights: [], commutes: [],
    jumpseats: [], trainingEvents: [], manualEvents: [], eventCategories: [],
  }

  constructor(
    private readonly anchor: number,
    private readonly updatedAt = '2026-10-01T00:00:00.000Z',
  ) {}

  iso(minutes: number): string {
    return new Date(this.anchor + minutes * MIN).toISOString()
  }

  pilot(firstName: string | null, base: string | null, home: string | null, extra: Partial<PilotInfoRow> = {}): this {
    this.rows.pilotInfos.push({
      first_name: firstName,
      base_raw_value: base,
      home_airport_code: home,
      is_active: true,
      updated_at: this.updatedAt,
      ...extra,
    })
    return this
  }

  trip(id: string, spec: TripSpec): this {
    const starts = spec.dutyPeriods.map((d) => d.start)
    const ends = spec.dutyPeriods.map((d) => d.end)
    this.rows.trips.push({
      id,
      start_date: this.iso(Math.min(...starts)),
      end_date: this.iso(Math.max(...ends)),
      days: spec.days ?? null,
      base: spec.base ?? null,
      updated_at: this.updatedAt,
    })
    spec.dutyPeriods.forEach((dp, i) => {
      const dpId = `${id}|dp|${i}`
      this.rows.dutyPeriods.push({
        id: dpId, trip_id: id, day: i + 1,
        start_time: this.iso(dp.start), end_time: this.iso(dp.end), updated_at: this.updatedAt,
      })
      dp.flights.forEach((f, j) => {
        this.rows.flights.push({
          id: `${id}|f|${i}.${j}`,
          trip_id: id,
          duty_period_id: dpId,
          flight_number: f.number === undefined ? '1000' : f.number,
          airline_code: f.airline ?? null,
          departure: f.dep,
          arrival: f.arr,
          scheduled_block_out_time: this.iso(f.out),
          scheduled_block_in_time: this.iso(f.in),
          block_out_time: this.iso(f.actualOut ?? f.out),
          block_in_time: this.iso(f.actualIn ?? f.in),
          is_dh: f.dh ?? false,
          is_commercial_deadhead: f.commercialDh ?? false,
          is_ground_transport: f.ground ?? false,
          hidden_from_timeline: f.hidden ?? false,
          updated_at: this.updatedAt,
        })
      })
    })
    return this
  }

  commute(id: string, from: string, to: string, direction: 'toHome' | 'toWork', dep: number, arr: number,
    sourceTripId: string | null = null, hidden = false): this {
    this.rows.commutes.push({
      id, from_airport: from, to_airport: to, direction,
      departure_time_zulu: this.iso(dep), arrival_time_zulu: this.iso(arr),
      source_trip_id: sourceTripId, hidden_from_timeline: hidden, updated_at: this.updatedAt,
    })
    return this
  }

  jumpseat(id: string, origin: string, destination: string, dep: number, arr: number,
    opts: { airline?: string | null; number?: string | null; sourceTripId?: string | null; status?: string; hidden?: boolean } = {}): this {
    this.rows.jumpseats.push({
      id,
      source_trip_id: opts.sourceTripId ?? null,
      flight_number: opts.number === undefined ? '1234' : opts.number,
      airline_code: opts.airline === undefined ? 'DL' : opts.airline,
      origin,
      destination,
      departure_time_zulu: this.iso(dep),
      arrival_time_zulu: this.iso(arr),
      status_raw_value: opts.status ?? 'Confirmed',
      is_commercial_deadhead: false,
      hidden_from_timeline: opts.hidden ?? false,
      updated_at: this.updatedAt,
    })
    return this
  }

  category(id: string, name: string): this {
    this.rows.eventCategories.push({ id, name })
    return this
  }

  manualEvent(id: string, title: string | null, start: number, end: number, location: string | null,
    opts: { parentId?: string; categoryId?: string; hidden?: boolean } = {}): this {
    this.rows.manualEvents.push({
      id, parent_event_id: opts.parentId ?? null, category_id: opts.categoryId ?? null, title,
      start_time_utc: this.iso(start), end_time_utc: this.iso(end), location,
      hidden_from_timeline: opts.hidden ?? false, updated_at: this.updatedAt,
    })
    return this
  }

  training(id: string, eventType: string | null, start: number, end: number, station: string | null, hidden = false): this {
    this.rows.trainingEvents.push({
      id, event_type: eventType, start_time_utc: this.iso(start), end_time_utc: this.iso(end),
      station, hidden_from_timeline: hidden, updated_at: this.updatedAt,
    })
    return this
  }

  build(fetchedAtMinutes = 0): RawSchedule {
    const rows = structuredClone(this.rows)
    return { ...rows, fetchedAt: this.anchor + fetchedAtMinutes * MIN, newestUpdatedAt: newestUpdatedAt(rows) }
  }
}
```

### Task 6: Demo timeline

This is the demo pilot "Alex" (§4.7). Engine and copy tests reuse this timeline with the fixed anchor `A`, so a test reads like "the moment the demo's `flying` scene shows".

Timeline, in minutes from the main trip's report time (A = Fri 9 Oct 2026 05:30 EDT):

| Minutes | What happens |
|---|---|
| −8640 … −7020 | T0, SDF→MIA (layover) MIA→SDF. Then the drive home SDF→LEX from −7020 to −6950. |
| −90 … −20 | Drive to work, LEX→SDF |
| 0 | Report at SDF (T1) |
| 60–150 | UPS 2961, SDF→RFD |
| 150–225 | Turn at RFD |
| 225–555 | UPS 2963, RFD→ANC |
| 555 … | Layover in Anchorage |
| 1560→1860 sched, 1600→1900 actual | UPS 2962, ANC→SDF, 40 minutes late. `demoRawForScene` includes the delay only from the `delayed` scene on (`DELAY_KNOWN_AT`, added in review). |
| 1890→1960 | Drive home as stored. The engine shifts it +40, to 1930→2000. |
| 4320–5040 | Reserve "RSVA" at SDF |
| 5760–6240 | "CQ" training at SDF |
| 10080 … | T2, SDF→ONT→SDF, with its own drives |

In the jumpseater variant (home MCO), the five drives become three Delta jumpseats:
- SDF→MCO from −6960 to −6840
- MCO→SDF from −300 to −180
- SDF→MCO from 2040 to 2160

**Files:**
- Create: `src/data/demo.ts`
- Test: `tests/data/demo.test.ts`

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { DEFAULT_SCENE, DEMO_SCENES, demoRaw, demoRawForScene, isDemoScene } from '../../src/data/demo'

const A = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 9, 30)

describe('demoRaw', () => {
  it('builds Alex the driver with three trips and five drives', () => {
    const raw = demoRaw('driver', A)
    expect(raw.pilotInfos[0]).toMatchObject({ first_name: 'Alex', base_raw_value: 'SDF', home_airport_code: 'LEX' })
    expect(raw.trips.map((t) => t.id)).toEqual(['demo-t0', 'demo-t1', 'demo-t2'])
    expect(raw.commutes).toHaveLength(5)
    expect(raw.jumpseats).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('swaps the drives for Delta jumpseats in the jumpseater variant', () => {
    const raw = demoRaw('jumpseater', A)
    expect(raw.pilotInfos[0].home_airport_code).toBe('MCO')
    expect(raw.commutes).toHaveLength(0)
    expect(raw.jumpseats.map((j) => `${j.origin}-${j.destination}`)).toEqual(['SDF-MCO', 'MCO-SDF', 'SDF-MCO'])
  })

  it('keeps the ANC→SDF flight 40 minutes late', () => {
    const f = demoRaw('driver', A).flights.find((x) => x.flight_number === '2962')!
    expect(Date.parse(f.block_out_time!) - Date.parse(f.scheduled_block_out_time!)).toBe(40 * 60_000)
  })
})

  it('positions the timeline so that now lands on the scene', () => {
    const now = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 20, 12)
    const raw = demoRawForScene('flying', now)
    const report = Date.parse(raw.trips.find((t) => t.id === 'demo-t1')!.start_date!)
    expect(now - report).toBe(DEMO_SCENES.flying.offset * 60_000)
    expect(raw.fetchedAt).toBe(now)
    expect(raw.newestUpdatedAt).toBe(now - 3 * 3_600_000)
  })

  it('validates scene names', () => {
    expect(isDemoScene('layover')).toBe(true)
    expect(isDemoScene('nope')).toBe(false)
    expect(isDemoScene(null)).toBe(false)
    expect(DEFAULT_SCENE).toBe('flying')
  })
})
```

- [x] **Step 3: Write `src/data/demo.ts`**

```ts
import { RawBuilder } from './rowBuilder'
import type { RawSchedule } from './rows'

export type DemoScene =
  | 'home' | 'driving-to-work' | 'base' | 'flying' | 'turn' | 'layover' | 'delayed'
  | 'last-leg' | 'driving-home' | 'commuting-home' | 'reserve' | 'training'

export type DemoVariant = 'driver' | 'jumpseater'

/** Which variant each scene uses, and how many minutes after the main trip's report "now" falls. */
export const DEMO_SCENES: Record<DemoScene, { variant: DemoVariant; offset: number }> = {
  home: { variant: 'driver', offset: -1440 },
  'driving-to-work': { variant: 'driver', offset: -60 },
  base: { variant: 'driver', offset: 30 },
  flying: { variant: 'driver', offset: 100 },
  turn: { variant: 'driver', offset: 180 },
  layover: { variant: 'driver', offset: -7800 },
  delayed: { variant: 'driver', offset: 1300 },
  'last-leg': { variant: 'driver', offset: 1700 },
  'driving-home': { variant: 'driver', offset: 1950 },
  'commuting-home': { variant: 'jumpseater', offset: 1960 },
  reserve: { variant: 'driver', offset: 4500 },
  training: { variant: 'driver', offset: 5900 },
}

export const DEFAULT_SCENE: DemoScene = 'flying'

export function isDemoScene(value: string | null): value is DemoScene {
  return value != null && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(DEMO_SCENES, value)
}

/**
 * Alex's schedule, in minutes around `anchor` (the report time of the main trip, demo-t1).
 * driver: home LEX, base SDF, drives to work. jumpseater: home MCO, rides Delta jumpseats.
 */
export function demoRaw(variant: DemoVariant, anchor: number): RawSchedule {
  const b = new RawBuilder(anchor)
  b.pilot('Alex', 'SDF', variant === 'driver' ? 'LEX' : 'MCO')

  b.trip('demo-t0', {
    days: 2,
    base: 'SDF',
    dutyPeriods: [
      { start: -8640, end: -8400, flights: [{ dep: 'SDF', arr: 'MIA', out: -8580, in: -8430, number: '2950' }] },
      { start: -7260, end: -7020, flights: [{ dep: 'MIA', arr: 'SDF', out: -7200, in: -7050, number: '2951' }] },
    ],
  })
  b.trip('demo-t1', {
    days: 2,
    base: 'SDF',
    dutyPeriods: [
      {
        start: 0,
        end: 585,
        flights: [
          { dep: 'SDF', arr: 'RFD', out: 60, in: 150, number: '2961' },
          { dep: 'RFD', arr: 'ANC', out: 225, in: 555, number: '2963' },
        ],
      },
      {
        start: 1500,
        end: 1930,
        flights: [{ dep: 'ANC', arr: 'SDF', out: 1560, in: 1860, actualOut: 1600, actualIn: 1900, number: '2962' }],
      },
    ],
  })
  b.trip('demo-t2', {
    days: 2,
    base: 'SDF',
    dutyPeriods: [
      { start: 10080, end: 10470, flights: [{ dep: 'SDF', arr: 'ONT', out: 10140, in: 10440, number: '2970' }] },
      { start: 11400, end: 11730, flights: [{ dep: 'ONT', arr: 'SDF', out: 11460, in: 11700, number: '2971' }] },
    ],
  })

  if (variant === 'driver') {
    b.commute('demo-c0-home', 'SDF', 'LEX', 'toHome', -7020, -6950, 'demo-t0')
    b.commute('demo-c1-work', 'LEX', 'SDF', 'toWork', -90, -20, 'demo-t1')
    b.commute('demo-c1-home', 'SDF', 'LEX', 'toHome', 1890, 1960, 'demo-t1')
    b.commute('demo-c2-work', 'LEX', 'SDF', 'toWork', 9990, 10060, 'demo-t2')
    b.commute('demo-c2-home', 'SDF', 'LEX', 'toHome', 11730, 11800, 'demo-t2')
  } else {
    b.jumpseat('demo-j0-home', 'SDF', 'MCO', -6960, -6840, { airline: 'DL', number: '1230' })
    b.jumpseat('demo-j1-work', 'MCO', 'SDF', -300, -180, { airline: 'DL', number: '1234' })
    b.jumpseat('demo-j1-home', 'SDF', 'MCO', 2040, 2160, { airline: 'DL', number: '1235' })
  }

  b.manualEvent('demo-rsv', 'RSVA', 4320, 5040, 'SDF')
  b.training('demo-cq', 'CQ', 5760, 6240, 'SDF')
  return b.build()
}

/** The demo schedule positioned so that `now` lands on `scene`. */
export function demoRawForScene(scene: DemoScene, now: number): RawSchedule {
  const { variant, offset } = DEMO_SCENES[scene]
  const raw = demoRaw(variant, now - offset * 60_000)
  return { ...raw, fetchedAt: now, newestUpdatedAt: now - 3 * 3_600_000 }
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import {
  agoText, ageText, arrivalWhenText, cardCountdown, clockText, daysText, delayText, departureText,
  leaveWhenText, longDateTimeText, narrativeCountdown, ordinalSuffix, timeWithZone, tripDepartsText,
} from '../../src/copy/format'

const NY = 'America/New_York'
const MIN = 60_000
const H = 60 * MIN
const D = 24 * H

describe('ordinalSuffix', () => {
  it.each([[1, 'st'], [2, 'nd'], [3, 'rd'], [4, 'th'], [11, 'th'], [12, 'th'], [13, 'th'], [21, 'st'], [22, 'nd'], [23, 'rd'], [31, 'st']])(
    '%i → %s', (day, suffix) => expect(ordinalSuffix(day)).toBe(suffix),
  )
})

describe('daysText', () => {
  it('pluralizes', () => {
    expect(daysText(1)).toBe('1 day')
    expect(daysText(4)).toBe('4 days')
  })
})

describe('narrativeCountdown', () => {
  it.each([
    [0, 'now!'], [-5000, 'now!'], [30_000, '<1m'], [59_999, '<1m'], [8 * MIN, '8m'],
    [8 * H + 8 * MIN, '8h 8m'], [D + 5 * MIN, '1d 0h 5m'], [2 * D + 8 * H + 8 * MIN, '2d 8h 8m'],
  ])('%i ms → %s', (ms, text) => expect(narrativeCountdown(ms)).toBe(text))
})

describe('cardCountdown', () => {
  it('pads every unit after the first and switches to seconds under an hour', () => {
    expect(cardCountdown(2 * D + 8 * H + 8 * MIN)).toEqual([
      { value: '2', unit: 'd' }, { value: '08', unit: 'h' }, { value: '08', unit: 'm' },
    ])
    expect(cardCountdown(8 * H + 5 * MIN)).toEqual([{ value: '8', unit: 'h' }, { value: '05', unit: 'm' }])
    expect(cardCountdown(7 * MIN + 5000)).toEqual([{ value: '7', unit: 'm' }, { value: '05', unit: 's' }])
    expect(cardCountdown(42_000)).toEqual([{ value: '0', unit: 'm' }, { value: '42', unit: 's' }])
  })

  it('returns null at zero, meaning "Now!"', () => expect(cardCountdown(0)).toBeNull())
})

describe('ageText and agoText', () => {
  it.each([[3000, null], [42_000, '42s'], [187_000, '3m 07s'], [7_500_000, '2h 05m'], [25 * H, '>1 day']])(
    '%i ms → %s', (ms, text) => expect(ageText(ms)).toBe(text),
  )

  it('says "just now" under five seconds', () => {
    expect(agoText('Checked', 2000)).toBe('Checked just now')
    expect(agoText('Checked', 42_000)).toBe('Checked 42s ago')
  })
})

describe('delayText', () => {
  it.each([[150, '2 hours 30 minutes'], [60, '1 hour'], [45, '45 minutes'], [61, '1 hour 1 minute'], [1, '1 minute'], [-40, '40 minutes']])(
    '%i → %s', (minutes, text) => expect(delayText(minutes)).toBe(text),
  )
})

describe('clock times', () => {
  const afternoon = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 17, 10) // 1:10pm EDT

  it('prints 12-hour lowercase or 24-hour', () => {
    expect(clockText(afternoon, NY, false)).toBe('1:10pm')
    expect(clockText(afternoon, NY, true)).toBe('13:10')
    expect(clockText(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 4, 5), NY, false)).toBe('12:05am')
    expect(clockText(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 4, 5), NY, true)).toBe('00:05')
    expect(clockText(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 16, 0), NY, false)).toBe('12:00pm')
  })

  it('adds the zone for {T}', () => {
    expect(timeWithZone(afternoon, NY, false)).toBe('1:10pm EDT')
    expect(timeWithZone(afternoon, 'Europe/Berlin', true)).toBe('19:10 GMT+2')
  })
})

describe('departureText', () => {
  const now = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 8, 13, 30) // Thu 8 Oct, 9:30am EDT

  it('says today, tomorrow, or the weekday and date', () => {
    expect(departureText(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 8, 22, 0), now, NY, false)).toBe('today at 6:00pm')
    expect(departureText(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 10, 30), now, NY, false)).toBe('tomorrow at 6:30am')
    expect(departureText(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 10, 30), now, NY, true)).toBe('tomorrow at 06:30')
    expect(departureText(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 11, 10, 30), now, NY, false)).toBe('Sunday the 11th at 6:30am')
  })

  it('compares calendar days in the viewer’s zone', () => {
    const lateNight = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 8, 3, 30) // Wed 7 Oct, 11:30pm EDT
    expect(departureText(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 8, 10, 30), lateNight, NY, false)).toBe('tomorrow at 6:30am')

describe('long dates', () => {

  })
})
```

- [x] **Step 3: Write `src/copy/format.ts`**

```ts
import { calendarDay, MONTHS, WEEKDAYS, zoneAbbr, zonedParts } from '../time/zoned'

const pad2 = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0')

/** "st" | "nd" | "rd" | "th" (Crewluve ordinalSuffix). */
export function ordinalSuffix(day: number): string {
  const lastTwo = day % 100
  if (lastTwo >= 11 && lastTwo <= 13) return 'th'
  switch (day % 10) {
    case 1: return 'st'
    case 2: return 'nd'
    case 3: return 'rd'
    default: return 'th'
  }
}

export function daysText(n: number): string {
  return `${n} ${n === 1 ? 'day' : 'days'}`
}

/** Narrative countdown (NarrativeCardView.countdownText): "2d 8h 8m", "8h 8m", "8m", "<1m", "now!". */
export function narrativeCountdown(ms: number): string {
  if (ms <= 0) return 'now!'
  const s = Math.floor(ms / 1000)
  const d = Math.floor(s / 86400)
  const h = Math.floor((s % 86400) / 3600)
  const m = Math.floor((s % 3600) / 60)
  if (d > 0) return `${d}d ${h}h ${m}m`
  if (h > 0) return `${h}h ${m}m`
  if (m > 0) return `${m}m`
  return '<1m'
}

export interface CountdownPart {
  value: string
  unit: 'd' | 'h' | 'm' | 's'
}

/** Big countdown (CountdownCardView): "2d 08h 08m", "8h 08m", "7m 05s". Null means "Now!". */
export function cardCountdown(ms: number): CountdownPart[] | null {
  if (ms <= 0) return null
  const s = Math.floor(ms / 1000)
  const d = Math.floor(s / 86400)
  const h = Math.floor((s % 86400) / 3600)
  const m = Math.floor((s % 3600) / 60)
  if (d > 0) return [{ value: String(d), unit: 'd' }, { value: pad2(h), unit: 'h' }, { value: pad2(m), unit: 'm' }]
  if (h > 0) return [{ value: String(h), unit: 'h' }, { value: pad2(m), unit: 'm' }]
  return [{ value: String(m), unit: 'm' }, { value: pad2(s % 60), unit: 's' }]
}

/** Footer age (SyncStatusBarView.elapsedText). Null under 5 s, meaning "just now". */
export function ageText(ms: number): string | null {
  const s = Math.max(0, Math.floor(ms / 1000))
  if (s < 5) return null
  const h = Math.floor(s / 3600)
  const m = Math.floor((s % 3600) / 60)
  if (h >= 24) return '>1 day'
  if (h > 0) return `${h}h ${pad2(m)}m`
  if (m > 0) return `${m}m ${pad2(s % 60)}s`
  return `${s}s`
}

export function agoText(prefix: string, ms: number): string {
  const age = ageText(ms)
  return age == null ? `${prefix} just now` : `${prefix} ${age} ago`
}

/** "2 hours 30 minutes", "1 hour", "45 minutes" (formattedDelayDuration). The sign is ignored. */
export function delayText(minutes: number): string {
  const total = Math.abs(Math.round(minutes))
  const h = Math.floor(total / 60)
  const m = total % 60
  const hours = `${h} ${h === 1 ? 'hour' : 'hours'}`
  const mins = `${m} ${m === 1 ? 'minute' : 'minutes'}`
  if (h > 0 && m > 0) return `${hours} ${mins}`
  return h > 0 ? hours : mins
}

/** "1:00pm" (12-hour, lowercase, no space: Crewluve "h:mma") or "13:00" ("HH:mm"). */
export function clockText(ms: number, timeZone: string, h24: boolean): string {
  const { hour, minute } = zonedParts(ms, timeZone)
  if (h24) return `${pad2(hour)}:${pad2(minute)}`
  return `${hour % 12 === 0 ? 12 : hour % 12}:${pad2(minute)}${hour < 12 ? 'am' : 'pm'}`
}

/** {T}: "1:00pm EST" or "13:00 EST" (formattedLocalTime). */
export function timeWithZone(ms: number, timeZone: string, h24: boolean): string {
  return `${clockText(ms, timeZone, h24)} ${zoneAbbr(ms, timeZone)}`
}

/** {dep}: "today at 6:00am", "tomorrow at 6:00am", or "Sunday the 1st at 6:00am" (formattedDepartureDate). */
export function departureText(ms: number, now: number, timeZone: string, h24: boolean): string {
  const time = clockText(ms, timeZone, h24)
  const diff = calendarDay(ms, timeZone) - calendarDay(now, timeZone)
  if (diff === 0) return `today at ${time}`
  if (diff === 1) return `tomorrow at ${time}`
  const p = zonedParts(ms, timeZone)
  return `${WEEKDAYS[p.weekday]} the ${p.day}${ordinalSuffix(p.day)} at ${time}`
}

function longDate(ms: number, timeZone: string): string {
  const p = zonedParts(ms, timeZone)
  return `${WEEKDAYS[p.weekday]} ${MONTHS[p.month - 1]} ${p.day}${ordinalSuffix(p.day)}`
}

/** {HB} date: "Monday October 12th at 8:02pm EDT" (homecomingText). */
export function longDateTimeText(ms: number, timeZone: string, h24: boolean): string {
  return `${longDate(ms, timeZone)} at ${timeWithZone(ms, timeZone, h24)}`
}

/** Arrival card date: "Sun, Mar 1 at 5:57 PM", with iOS's narrow no-break space before PM; or "Sun, Mar 1 at 17:57". */
export function arrivalWhenText(ms: number, timeZone: string, h24: boolean): string {
  const p = zonedParts(ms, timeZone)
  const time = h24
    ? `${pad2(p.hour)}:${pad2(p.minute)}`
    : `${p.hour % 12 === 0 ? 12 : p.hour % 12}:${pad2(p.minute)}\u202F${p.hour < 12 ? 'AM' : 'PM'}`
  return `${WEEKDAYS[p.weekday].slice(0, 3)}, ${MONTHS[p.month - 1].slice(0, 3)} ${p.day} at ${time}`
}

/** Leave card subtitle: "Departs Sunday March 1st at 5:57pm" (24-hour: "… at 17:57", fixing the app's "17:57pm"). */
export function leaveWhenText(ms: number, timeZone: string, h24: boolean): string {
  return `Departs ${longDate(ms, timeZone)} at ${clockText(ms, timeZone, h24)}`
}

/** Commuter with no logged commute: "Trip departs Louisville Sunday March 1st at 5:57pm". */
export function tripDepartsText(city: string, ms: number, timeZone: string, h24: boolean): string {
  return `Trip departs ${city} ${longDate(ms, timeZone)} at ${clockText(ms, timeZone, h24)}`
}
```

```ts
    expect(airlineColor('WN')).toBe('#FF8000')

    expect(flightPhrase('5X', '2961')).toBe(' on UPS flight 2961')
    expect(flightPhrase('DL', '1234')).toBe(' on Delta flight 1234')
    expect(flightPhrase(null, null)).toBe('')
  })
})
```

```ts
import type { IconName } from '../icons/iconPaths'
import type { ColorToken } from './colors'

const ICAO_TO_IATA: Record<string, string> = {
  UPS: '5X', FDX: 'FX', AAL: 'AA', DAL: 'DL', UAL: 'UA', SWA: 'WN', JBU: 'B6', NKS: 'NK',
  FFT: 'F9', ASA: 'AS', HAL: 'HA', SKW: 'OO', RPA: 'YX', ENY: 'MQ', PSA: 'OH',
}

const NAMES: Record<string, string> = {
  WN: 'Southwest', AA: 'American', DL: 'Delta', UA: 'United', B6: 'JetBlue', AS: 'Alaska', NK: 'Spirit',
  F9: 'Frontier', G4: 'Allegiant', MX: 'Breeze', HA: 'Hawaiian', XP: 'Avelo', SY: 'Sun Country',
  FX: 'FedEx', '5X': 'UPS', '5Y': 'Atlas Air', GG: 'Air Cargo Germany', K4: 'Kalitta', PO: 'Polar Air', GT: 'Air Inuit',
  AC: 'Air Canada', LH: 'Lufthansa', BA: 'British Airways', AF: 'Air France', CX: 'Cathay Pacific', KL: 'KLM',
  QF: 'Qantas', EK: 'Emirates', SQ: 'Singapore',
  NH: 'ANA', JL: 'Japan Airlines', KE: 'Korean Air', OZ: 'Asiana', CI: 'China Airlines', BR: 'EVA Air',
  TG: 'Thai Airways', MH: 'Malaysia',
  QR: 'Qatar', EY: 'Etihad', TK: 'Turkish',
  LX: 'Swiss', OS: 'Austrian', AZ: 'ITA Airways', IB: 'Iberia', SK: 'SAS', AY: 'Finnair', EI: 'Aer Lingus',
  VS: 'Virgin Atlantic', VA: 'Virgin Australia', NZ: 'Air New Zealand',
  AM: 'Aeromexico', LA: 'LATAM', CM: 'Copa', AV: 'Avianca',
  OO: 'SkyWest', YX: 'Republic', '9E': 'Endeavor', QX: 'Horizon', ZW: 'Air Wisconsin', MQ: 'Envoy', OH: 'PSA',
  CP: 'Compass', G7: 'GoJet', AX: 'Trans States',
}

// SF Symbol → Phosphor: heart.fill Heart, triangle.fill Triangle, star.fill Star, globe.americas.fill
// GlobeHemisphereWest, airplane.circle.fill AirplaneTilt, mountain.2.fill Mountains, dollarsign.circle.fill
// CurrencyCircleDollar, leaf.fill Leaf, sun.max.fill Sun, wind Wind, bird.fill Bird, shippingbox.fill Package,
// crown.fill Crown, hare.fill Rabbit, sparkles Sparkle, circle.lefthalf.filled CircleHalf,
// globe.asia.australia.fill GlobeHemisphereEast, diamond.fill Diamond, plus Plus, chevron.up.chevron.down
// CaretUpDown, sailboat.fill Sailboat, pawprint.fill PawPrint, location.north.fill NavigationArrow.
const SYMBOLS: Record<string, IconName> = {
  WN: 'Heart', DL: 'Triangle', AA: 'Star', UA: 'GlobeHemisphereWest', B6: 'AirplaneTilt', AS: 'Mountains',
  NK: 'CurrencyCircleDollar', F9: 'Leaf', G4: 'Sun', MX: 'Wind', HA: 'Leaf', XP: 'Bird', SY: 'Sun',
  FX: 'Package', '5X': 'Package', AC: 'Leaf', LH: 'Bird', BA: 'Crown', AF: 'AirplaneTilt', CX: 'Bird',
  KL: 'Crown', QF: 'Rabbit', EK: 'Sparkle', SQ: 'Bird', NH: 'AirplaneTilt', JL: 'Bird', KE: 'CircleHalf',
  OZ: 'AirplaneTilt', CI: 'Leaf', BR: 'GlobeHemisphereEast', TG: 'Leaf', MH: 'Diamond', QR: 'Rabbit',
  EY: 'Bird', TK: 'Bird', LX: 'Plus', OS: 'CaretUpDown', SK: 'Sailboat', EI: 'Leaf', VS: 'Sparkle',
  VA: 'Sparkle', NZ: 'Leaf', AM: 'Bird', CM: 'Star', AV: 'Bird', QX: 'Mountains', MQ: 'Star', OH: 'Star',
  CP: 'NavigationArrow', '5Y': 'GlobeHemisphereWest', PO: 'PawPrint',
}

const RGB: Record<string, [number, number, number]> = {
  WN: [1.0, 0.5, 0.0], AA: [0.0, 0.2, 0.5], DL: [0.0, 0.15, 0.4], UA: [0.0, 0.45, 0.7], B6: [0.0, 0.35, 0.65],
  AS: [0.0, 0.3, 0.5], NK: [1.0, 0.8, 0.0], F9: [0.0, 0.5, 0.3], G4: [1.0, 0.5, 0.0], SY: [1.0, 0.4, 0.0],
  MX: [0.0, 0.7, 0.7], HA: [0.5, 0.0, 0.5], XP: [1.0, 0.8, 0.0],
  FX: [0.5, 0.0, 0.5], K4: [0.8, 0.0, 0.0], '5Y': [0.0, 0.2, 0.6], GG: [0.5, 0.5, 0.5],
  AC: [0.8, 0.0, 0.0], LH: [0.0, 0.1, 0.4], BA: [0.0, 0.2, 0.5], AF: [0.0, 0.3, 0.5], CX: [0.0, 0.5, 0.4],
  KL: [0.0, 0.6, 0.9], QF: [0.9, 0.0, 0.0], EK: [0.9, 0.0, 0.2], SQ: [0.0, 0.2, 0.5],
  NH: [0.0, 0.27, 0.5], JL: [0.79, 0.15, 0.18], KE: [0.0, 0.39, 0.82], OZ: [0.93, 0.11, 0.14],
  CI: [0.4, 0.0, 0.4], BR: [0.0, 0.65, 0.32], TG: [0.36, 0.18, 0.57], MH: [0.0, 0.2, 0.4],
  QR: [0.36, 0.02, 0.2], EY: [0.77, 0.65, 0.47], TK: [0.78, 0.05, 0.19],
  LX: [0.84, 0.1, 0.13], OS: [0.89, 0.02, 0.08], AZ: [0.0, 0.2, 0.6], IB: [0.85, 0.12, 0.02],
  SK: [0.0, 0.0, 0.5], AY: [0.04, 0.06, 0.38], EI: [0.0, 0.42, 0.33], VS: [0.88, 0.04, 0.04], VA: [0.6, 0.0, 0.2],
  NZ: [0.0, 0.71, 0.68],
  AM: [0.0, 0.15, 0.3], LA: [0.11, 0.06, 0.42], CM: [0.0, 0.22, 0.46], AV: [0.89, 0.02, 0.08],
  OO: [0.0, 0.3, 0.6], YX: [0.0, 0.3, 0.5], '9E': [0.0, 0.2, 0.4], QX: [0.0, 0.5, 0.3], ZW: [0.8, 0.0, 0.2],
  MQ: [0.0, 0.2, 0.5], OH: [0.0, 0.2, 0.5], CP: [0.0, 0.15, 0.4], G7: [0.0, 0.45, 0.7], AX: [0.0, 0.3, 0.5],
  PO: [0.0, 0.3, 0.6], GT: [0.8, 0.0, 0.0],
}

const resolve = (code: string) => ICAO_TO_IATA[code.trim().toUpperCase()] ?? code.trim().toUpperCase()

const toHex = ([r, g, b]: [number, number, number]) =>
  `#${[r, g, b].map((c) => Math.round(c * 255).toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('').toUpperCase()}` as const

export function airlineName(code: string | null): string {
  if (!code) return 'Commercial'
  const c = resolve(code)
  return NAMES[c] ?? c
}

export function airlineIcon(code: string | null): IconName {
  if (!code) return 'Airplane'
  return SYMBOLS[resolve(code)] ?? 'Airplane'
}

export function airlineColor(code: string | null): ColorToken {
  if (!code) return 'blue'
  const c = resolve(code)
  if (c === '5X') return 'ups' // #663300 light, #E09E33 dark (the only theme-aware brand color)
  const rgb = RGB[c]
  return rgb ? (toHex(rgb) as ColorToken) : 'blue'
}

/** {FL}: " on UPS flight 2961"; empty when there is no usable (all-digit) flight number. */
export function flightPhrase(airline: string | null, number: string | null): string {
  return number ? ` on ${airlineName(airline)} flight ${number}` : ''
}
```

```ts
    .commute('c-orphan', 'SDF', 'LEX', 'toHome', 900, 970, 'gone')
    .jumpseat('j-dup', 'SDF', 'MCO', 500, 600, { sourceTripId: 't1' })
    .jumpseat('j1', 'MCO', 'SDF', 700, 800, { airline: null, number: '0451' })
    .manualEvent('m-sub', 'TEC part 2', 1000, 1100, 'SDF', { parentId: 'm-parent' })
    .manualEvent('m-hidden', 'Secret', 1000, 1100, 'SDF', { hidden: true })
    .manualEvent('m1', 'RSVB 0600', 1200, 1900, ' sdf ')
    .training('te1', 'PC', 2000, 2480, 'SDF')
    .build()
  const s = buildSchedule(raw, A)

  it('keeps visible flights with effective times, the company airline, and clean numbers', () => {
    const flights = s.trips[0].dutyPeriods[0].flights
    expect(flights.map((f) => f.number)).toEqual(['81', null])
    expect(flights[0]).toMatchObject({ airline: '5X', deadhead: false, ground: false, start: A + 60 * M })
    expect(flights[1]).toMatchObject({ airline: null, deadhead: true, start: A + 240 * M, schedStart: A + 200 * M })
  })

  it('shifts the drive home by the trip’s last-flight delay and drops commutes whose trip is gone', () => {
    expect(s.commutes).toEqual([
      { id: 'c-home', from: 'SDF', to: 'LEX', toHome: true, start: A + 380 * M, end: A + 450 * M, schedEnd: A + 410 * M, sourceTripId: 't1' },
    ])
  })

  it('drops jumpseats that duplicate a trip deadhead', () => {
    expect(s.jumpseats.map((j) => j.id)).toEqual(['j1'])
    expect(s.jumpseats[0]).toMatchObject({ airline: '5X', number: '451', deadhead: true, tripId: null })
  })

  it('drops sub-events and hidden events, and classifies the rest', () => {
    expect(s.events.map((e) => [e.id, e.kind, e.title, e.airport])).toEqual([
      ['m1', 'reserve', 'RSV-B', 'SDF'],
      ['te1', 'training', 'PC Training', 'SDF'],
    ])
  })

  it('drops trips without live duty periods, and flags accounts with no data', () => {
    const bare = new RawBuilder(A).build()
    bare.trips.push({ id: 'bare', start_date: new Date(A).toISOString(), end_date: new Date(A + H).toISOString(), days: 1, base: 'SDF', updated_at: '2026-10-01T00:00:00Z' })
    const s2 = buildSchedule(bare, A)
    expect(s2.trips).toEqual([])
    expect(s2.empty).toBe(false)
    expect(buildSchedule(new RawBuilder(A).build(), A).empty).toBe(true)
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/engine/types.ts`**

```ts
/** 3-letter IATA airport code. */
export type Iata = string

export interface Pilot {
  firstName: string | null
  base: Iata | null
  home: Iata | null
  /** home ?? base: how Crewlu treats a pilot who never set a home airport. */
  effHome: Iata | null
}

/** A timed movement between airports: a trip flight or a standalone jumpseat. Times are epoch ms. */
export interface Move {
  id: string
  tripId: string | null
  dep: Iata
  arr: Iata
  /** Effective (delay-aware) block out and in. */
  start: number
  end: number
  schedStart: number
  schedEnd: number
  /** Airline code; company flights resolve to '5X' (UPS). */
  airline: string | null
  /** Digits only; null for placeholders such as XXXX, CML, GND. */
  number: string | null
  deadhead: boolean
  ground: boolean
}

export interface DutyPeriod {
  id: string
  tripId: string
  start: number
  end: number
  flights: Move[]
}

export interface Trip {
  id: string
  start: number
  end: number
  days: number | null
  base: Iata | null
  dutyPeriods: DutyPeriod[]
}

export interface Commute {
  id: string
  from: Iata
  to: Iata
  toHome: boolean
  start: number
  end: number
  /** The stored arrival, before any late-flight shift. */
  schedEnd: number
  sourceTripId: string | null
}

export type DutyEventKind = 'reserve' | 'hotStandby' | 'training' | 'event'

export interface DutyEvent {
  id: string
  kind: DutyEventKind
  title: string
  airport: Iata | null
  start: number
  end: number
}

export interface Schedule {
  pilot: Pilot
  trips: Trip[]
  jumpseats: Move[]
  commutes: Commute[]
  events: DutyEvent[]
  fetchedAt: number
  newestUpdatedAt: number | null
  /** No pilot profile and no trips at all: CrewLu Cloud has nothing for this account. */
  empty: boolean
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/engine/normalize.ts`**

```ts
import type { DutyPeriodRow, FlightRow, PilotInfoRow, RawSchedule } from '../data/rows'
import { DAY, HOUR } from '../time/zoned'
import type { Commute, DutyEvent, DutyEventKind, DutyPeriod, Iata, Move, Pilot, Schedule, Trip } from './types'

const MIN_VALID = Date.UTC(2000, 0, 1)
const MAX_AHEAD = 2 * 365 * DAY
/** A scheduled-vs-actual gap beyond this is bad data, not a delay (the Flight Globe's guard). */
const MAX_CRED_DELTA = 6 * HOUR
const MAX_BLOCK = 26 * HOUR
const RESERVE_CODES = ['RSVA', 'RSVB', 'RSVC', 'RSVD', 'RSCA', 'RSCP', 'RSSA', 'RSSB', 'RSSC', 'RSSD', 'LCO', 'RCID']

/** 3-letter IATA code, uppercased; a 4-letter base such as "SDFZ" becomes "SDF". */
export function toIata(code: string | null | undefined): Iata | null {
  const s = (code ?? '').trim().toUpperCase()
  if (/^[A-Z0-9]{3}$/.test(s)) return s
  if (/^[A-Z]{4}$/.test(s)) return s.slice(0, 3)
  return null
}

/** Epoch ms, or null for missing, unparseable, pre-2000, or more-than-2-years-ahead values. */
export function parseInstant(iso: string | null | undefined, now: number): number | null {
  if (!iso) return null
  const t = Date.parse(iso)
  if (!Number.isFinite(t) || t < MIN_VALID || t > now + MAX_AHEAD) return null
  return t
}

/** Digits only, leading zeros stripped ("0081" → "81"); null for placeholders like XXXX, CML, GND. */
export function normalizeFlightNumber(raw: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  const s = (raw ?? '').trim()
  if (!/^\d+$/.test(s)) return null
  return s.replace(/^0+(?=\d)/, '')
}

/** The airline code, or '5X' (UPS) for a company flight: no code, not ground transport, all-digit number. */
export function resolveAirline(airlineCode: string | null | undefined, rawNumber: string | null | undefined, ground: boolean): string | null {
  const code = (airlineCode ?? '').trim().toUpperCase()
  if (code) return code
  if (!ground && /^\d+$/.test((rawNumber ?? '').trim())) return '5X' // PartnerStatusGenerator.swift:321-325
  return null
}

/** Delay-aware block times: actual/ETD when believable, otherwise the schedule (spec §6.2). */
export function effectiveTimes(
  schedOut: number | null,
  schedIn: number | null,
  actOut: number | null,
  actIn: number | null,
): Pick<Move, 'start' | 'end' | 'schedStart' | 'schedEnd'> | null {
  const sOut = schedOut ?? actOut
  const sIn = schedIn ?? actIn
  if (sOut == null || sIn == null) return null
  const credible = (actual: number | null, scheduled: number) =>
    actual != null && Math.abs(actual - scheduled) <= MAX_CRED_DELTA ? actual : scheduled
  let start = credible(actOut, sOut)
  let end = credible(actIn, sIn)
  if (end <= start || end - start > MAX_BLOCK) {
    start = sOut
    end = sIn
  }
  if (end <= start || end - start > MAX_BLOCK) return null
  return { start, end, schedStart: sOut, schedEnd: sIn }
}

/** The most recently updated active profile row (16 accounts have two). */
export function pickPilot(rows: PilotInfoRow[]): Pilot {
  const row = rows
    .filter((r) => r.is_active !== false)
    .sort((a, b) => Date.parse(b.updated_at) - Date.parse(a.updated_at))[0]
  const base = toIata(row?.base_raw_value)
  const home = toIata(row?.home_airport_code)
  return { firstName: row?.first_name?.trim() || null, base, home, effHome: home ?? base }
}

/** Duty's manualEventLegType + manualEventLabel (PartnerStatusGenerator.swift:894-929). */
export function classifyManualEvent(title: string | null, categoryName: string | null): { kind: Exclude<DutyEventKind, 'training'>; title: string } {
  const text = (title ?? '').trim()
  const upper = text.toUpperCase()
  if (upper.includes('HOT STANDBY')) return { kind: 'hotStandby', title: 'Hot Standby' }
  const code = RESERVE_CODES.find((c) => upper.includes(c))
  if (code) return { kind: 'reserve', title: code.length >= 4 ? `${code.slice(0, 3)}-${code.slice(-1)}` : code }
  if ((categoryName ?? '').toLowerCase().includes('reserve')) return { kind: 'reserve', title: text || 'Manual Event' }
  return { kind: 'event', title: text || 'Manual Event' }
}

/** Duty's TrainingEvent.displayTitle: "\(eventType) Training". */
export function trainingTitle(eventType: string | null): string {
  return `${(eventType ?? '').trim()} Training`.trim()
}

/** CrewLu Cloud rows → a clean, delay-aware Schedule (spec §6.2–6.3). */
export function buildSchedule(raw: RawSchedule, now: number): Schedule {
  const pilot = pickPilot(raw.pilotInfos)
  const visible = (r: { hidden_from_timeline: boolean | null }) => r.hidden_from_timeline !== true

  const flightsByDutyPeriod = groupBy(raw.flights.filter(visible), (f) => f.duty_period_id)
  const dutyPeriodsByTrip = groupBy(raw.dutyPeriods, (d) => d.trip_id)

  const trips: Trip[] = []
  for (const row of raw.trips) {
    const start = parseInstant(row.start_date, now)
    const end = parseInstant(row.end_date, now)
    if (start == null || end == null) continue
    const dutyPeriods = (dutyPeriodsByTrip.get(row.id) ?? [])
      .map((dp) => toDutyPeriod(dp, row.id, flightsByDutyPeriod.get(dp.id) ?? [], now))
      .filter((dp): dp is DutyPeriod => dp != null)
      .sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start)
    if (dutyPeriods.length === 0) continue // trips with no live duty periods are dropped
    trips.push({ id: row.id, start, end, days: row.days ?? null, base: toIata(row.base) ?? pilot.base, dutyPeriods })
  }
  trips.sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start)
  const tripById = new Map(trips.map((t) => [t.id, t]))

  const commutes: Commute[] = []
  for (const c of raw.commutes.filter(visible)) {
    const from = toIata(c.from_airport)
    const to = toIata(c.to_airport)
    const start = parseInstant(c.departure_time_zulu, now)
    const end = parseInstant(c.arrival_time_zulu, now)
    if (!from || !to || start == null || end == null || end <= start) continue
    const sourceTrip = c.source_trip_id ? tripById.get(c.source_trip_id) : undefined
    if (c.source_trip_id && !sourceTrip) continue // its trip is gone (CommuteCleanupService.swift:46)
    const toHome = c.direction === 'toHome' || (c.direction !== 'toWork' && to === pilot.effHome)
    // Crewlu writes commute times once, so move the drive home with a late (or early) last flight.
    const shift = toHome && sourceTrip ? lastFlightDelay(sourceTrip) : 0
    commutes.push({ id: c.id, from, to, toHome, start: start + shift, end: end + shift, schedEnd: end, sourceTripId: c.source_trip_id })
  }

  const jumpseats: Move[] = []
  for (const j of raw.jumpseats.filter(visible)) {
    if (j.source_trip_id) continue // duplicates a deadhead that is already in its trip
    const dep = toIata(j.origin)
    const arr = toIata(j.destination)
    const times = effectiveTimes(parseInstant(j.departure_time_zulu, now), parseInstant(j.arrival_time_zulu, now), null, null)
    if (!dep || !arr || !times) continue
    jumpseats.push({
      id: j.id, tripId: null, dep, arr, ...times,
      airline: resolveAirline(j.airline_code, j.flight_number, false),
      number: normalizeFlightNumber(j.flight_number),
      deadhead: true,
      ground: false,
    })
  }

  const categoryName = new Map(raw.eventCategories.map((c) => [c.id, c.name]))
  const events: DutyEvent[] = []
  for (const m of raw.manualEvents.filter(visible)) {
    if (m.parent_event_id) continue // TEC sub-events
    const start = parseInstant(m.start_time_utc, now)
    const end = parseInstant(m.end_time_utc, now)
    if (start == null || end == null || end <= start) continue
    const { kind, title } = classifyManualEvent(m.title, m.category_id ? categoryName.get(m.category_id) ?? null : null)
    events.push({ id: m.id, kind, title, airport: toIata(m.location), start, end })
  }
  for (const te of raw.trainingEvents.filter(visible)) {
    const start = parseInstant(te.start_time_utc, now)
    const end = parseInstant(te.end_time_utc, now)
    if (start == null || end == null || end <= start) continue
    events.push({ id: te.id, kind: 'training', title: trainingTitle(te.event_type), airport: toIata(te.station), start, end })
  }

  return {
    pilot,
    trips,
    jumpseats,
    commutes,
    events,
    fetchedAt: raw.fetchedAt,
    newestUpdatedAt: raw.newestUpdatedAt,
    empty: raw.pilotInfos.length === 0 && raw.trips.length === 0,
  }
}

function toDutyPeriod(row: DutyPeriodRow, tripId: string, flights: FlightRow[], now: number): DutyPeriod | null {
  const start = parseInstant(row.start_time, now)
  const end = parseInstant(row.end_time, now)
  if (start == null || end == null) return null
  const moves = flights
    .map((f) => toMove(f, tripId, now))
    .filter((m): m is Move => m != null)
    .sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start)
  return { id: row.id, tripId, start, end, flights: moves }
}

function toMove(row: FlightRow, tripId: string, now: number): Move | null {
  const dep = toIata(row.departure)
  const arr = toIata(row.arrival)
  const times = effectiveTimes(
    parseInstant(row.scheduled_block_out_time, now),
    parseInstant(row.scheduled_block_in_time, now),
    parseInstant(row.block_out_time, now),
    parseInstant(row.block_in_time, now),
  )
  if (!dep || !arr || !times) return null
  const rawNumber = (row.flight_number ?? '').trim().toUpperCase()
  const ground = row.is_ground_transport === true || rawNumber === 'GND'
  return {
    id: row.id,
    tripId,
    dep,
    arr,
    ...times,
    airline: resolveAirline(row.airline_code, row.flight_number, ground),
    number: normalizeFlightNumber(row.flight_number),
    deadhead: row.is_dh === true || row.is_commercial_deadhead === true || rawNumber === 'CML',
    ground,
  }
}

/** Effective minus scheduled block-in of the trip's last flight (effectiveTimes already rejected > ±6 h). */
function lastFlightDelay(trip: Trip): number {
  const flights = trip.dutyPeriods.flatMap((dp) => dp.flights)
  if (flights.length === 0) return 0
  const last = flights.reduce((a, b) => (b.end > a.end ? b : a))
  return last.end - last.schedEnd
}

function groupBy<T>(rows: T[], key: (row: T) => string | null): Map<string, T[]> {
  const map = new Map<string, T[]>()
  for (const row of rows) {
    const k = key(row)
    if (!k) continue
    const list = map.get(k)
    if (list) list.push(row)
    else map.set(k, [row])
  }
  return map
}
```

### Task 10: Legs

Duty's leg builder, run on CrewLu Cloud data (§6.3). The legs, in order:
- flights, turns, and the report window
- the gaps between duty periods (home, base, or layover)
- jumpseats, drives, and duty events

**Files:**
- Create: `src/engine/legs.ts`, `tests/fixtures/engine.ts`

```ts
import type { RawSchedule } from '../../src/data/rows'
import { buildLegs } from '../../src/engine/legs'
import { buildSchedule } from '../../src/engine/normalize'

/** Fri 9 Oct 2026, 5:30am EDT: the report time of the demo's main trip. */
export const A = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 9, 30)
export const M = 60_000
export const at = (minutes: number) => A + minutes * M

export function engineFor(raw: RawSchedule) {
  const schedule = buildSchedule(raw, A)
  return { schedule, legs: buildLegs(schedule) }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/engine/legs.ts`**

```ts
import type { DutyEventKind, Iata, Move, Schedule } from './types'

export type PlaceKind = 'report' | 'turn' | 'layover' | 'base' | 'home'

export interface MoveLeg extends Move {
  kind: 'flight' | 'jumpseat'
}

export interface DriveLeg {
  kind: 'drive'
  id: string
  tripId: null
  from: Iata
  to: Iata
  toHome: boolean
  start: number
  end: number
  schedEnd: number
}

export interface PlaceLeg {
  kind: PlaceKind
  id: string
  tripId: string
  airport: Iata
  start: number
  end: number
}

export interface DutyLeg {
  kind: DutyEventKind
  id: string
  tripId: null
  title: string
  airport: Iata | null
  start: number
  end: number
  /** An event titled like a drive ("Driving the truck home") counts as a drive for homecomings. */
  driveLike: boolean
}

export type Leg = MoveLeg | DriveLeg | PlaceLeg | DutyLeg

export const isMove = (l: Leg): l is MoveLeg => l.kind === 'flight' || l.kind === 'jumpseat'

export const isDuty = (l: Leg): l is DutyLeg =>
  l.kind === 'reserve' || l.kind === 'hotStandby' || l.kind === 'training' || l.kind === 'event'

/** Where the pilot is once this leg ends. */
export function arrivalOf(l: Leg): Iata | null {
  if (isMove(l)) return l.arr
  if (l.kind === 'drive') return l.to
  return l.airport
}

/** Where this leg begins. */
export function departureOf(l: Leg): Iata | null {
  if (isMove(l)) return l.dep
  if (l.kind === 'drive') return l.from
  return l.airport
}

/** A flight, jumpseat, drive, or drive-titled event that arrives at the pilot's home. */
export function isHomecomingMove(l: Leg, effHome: Iata | null): boolean {
  if (!effHome) return false
  const moving = isMove(l) || l.kind === 'drive' || (l.kind === 'event' && l.driveLike)
  return moving && arrivalOf(l) === effHome
}

/** Scheduled end, for delay colors. Moves and drives keep one; other legs never shift. */
export function schedEndOf(l: Leg): number {
  return isMove(l) || l.kind === 'drive' ? l.schedEnd : l.end
}

const DRIVE_TITLE = /driv|truck|car/i

/** Duty's leg builder (PartnerStatusGenerator.swift:707-1065) on CrewLu Cloud data, sorted by start. */
export function buildLegs(s: Schedule): Leg[] {
  const legs: Leg[] = []
  const { effHome } = s.pilot

  for (const trip of s.trips) {
    const base = trip.base ?? s.pilot.base
    const flown = trip.dutyPeriods.filter((dp) => dp.flights.length > 0)
    flown.forEach((dp, i) => {
      const flights = dp.flights
      for (const f of flights) legs.push({ ...f, kind: 'flight' })
      for (let j = 1; j < flights.length; j++) {
        const prev = flights[j - 1]
        const next = flights[j]
        if (next.start > prev.end) {
          legs.push({ kind: 'turn', id: `${dp.id}|turn|${j}`, tripId: trip.id, airport: prev.arr, start: prev.end, end: next.start })
        }
      }
      // Report (show) time to the first departure: "at base", not "home" (fixes PartnerStatusGenerator.swift:1327).
      if (i === 0 && dp.start < flights[0].start) {
        legs.push({ kind: 'report', id: `${trip.id}|report`, tripId: trip.id, airport: flights[0].dep, start: dp.start, end: flights[0].start })
      }
      if (i > 0) {
        const previous = flown[i - 1].flights
        const landed = previous[previous.length - 1]
        if (flights[0].start > landed.end) {
          const kind: PlaceKind = landed.arr === effHome ? 'home' : landed.arr === base ? 'base' : 'layover'
          legs.push({ kind, id: `${dp.id}|gap`, tripId: trip.id, airport: landed.arr, start: landed.end, end: flights[0].start })
        }
      }
    })
  }

  for (const j of s.jumpseats) legs.push({ ...j, kind: 'jumpseat' })
  for (const c of s.commutes) {
    legs.push({ kind: 'drive', id: c.id, tripId: null, from: c.from, to: c.to, toHome: c.toHome, start: c.start, end: c.end, schedEnd: c.schedEnd })
  }
  for (const e of s.events) {
    legs.push({
      kind: e.kind, id: e.id, tripId: null, title: e.title, airport: e.airport, start: e.start, end: e.end,
      driveLike: e.kind === 'event' && DRIVE_TITLE.test(e.title),
    })
  }

  trimPlacesAtMoves(legs)
  return legs.filter((l) => l.end > l.start).sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start || a.end - b.end)
}

/** A layover/base/home/turn/report leg stops where a move begins inside it (SharedPilotStatus.swift:199-236). */
function trimPlacesAtMoves(legs: Leg[]): void {
  const moveStarts = legs.filter(isMove).map((m) => m.start)
  for (const leg of legs) {
    if (isMove(leg) || isDuty(leg) || leg.kind === 'drive') continue
    for (const start of moveStarts) {
      if (start > leg.start && start < leg.end) leg.end = start
    }
  }
}
```

### Task 11: Status resolver

This is Crewluve's `TripStateResolver` (§6.4), delay-aware and with the off-trip fix. The tests port `CrewluvTests/TripStateResolverGapTests.swift`. They use hand-built legs measured in hours.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/engine/status.ts`, `tests/fixtures/legs.ts`
- Test: `tests/engine/status.test.ts`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the hand-built leg fixture `tests/fixtures/legs.ts`**

```ts
import type { DriveLeg, DutyLeg, MoveLeg, PlaceKind, PlaceLeg } from '../../src/engine/legs'
import type { DutyEventKind, Pilot } from '../../src/engine/types'

/** Hand-built legs use hours from 0 as their unit. */
export const H = 3_600_000

export const pilot = (home: string | null, base: string | null): Pilot => ({ firstName: 'Alex', base, home, effHome: home ?? base })

/** A trip flight (tripId given) or a standalone Delta jumpseat (tripId null). */
export function flight(dep: string, arr: string, start: number, end: number, tripId: string | null = 'T1', extra: Partial<MoveLeg> = {}): MoveLeg {
  return {
    kind: tripId ? 'flight' : 'jumpseat',
    id: `${tripId ?? 'js'}:${dep}-${arr}@${start}`,
    tripId,
    dep,
    arr,
    start: start * H,
    end: end * H,
    schedStart: start * H,
    schedEnd: end * H,
    airline: tripId ? '5X' : 'DL',
    number: '100',
    deadhead: !tripId,
    ground: false,
    ...extra,
  }
}

export function place(kind: PlaceKind, airport: string, start: number, end: number, tripId = 'T1'): PlaceLeg {
  return { kind, id: `${tripId}:${kind}@${start}`, tripId, airport, start: start * H, end: end * H }
}

export function drive(from: string, to: string, toHome: boolean, start: number, end: number): DriveLeg {
  return { kind: 'drive', id: `drive@${start}`, tripId: null, from, to, toHome, start: start * H, end: end * H, schedEnd: end * H }
}

export function duty(kind: DutyEventKind, title: string, airport: string | null, start: number, end: number): DutyLeg {
  return { kind, id: `${kind}@${start}`, tripId: null, title, airport, start: start * H, end: end * H, driveLike: false }
}

export const sorted = <T extends { start: number; end: number }>(legs: T[]) =>
  [...legs].sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start || a.end - b.end)
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { RawBuilder } from '../../src/data/rowBuilder'
import { resolveStatus } from '../../src/engine/status'
import { A, at, engineFor } from '../fixtures/engine'
import { H, drive, duty, flight, pilot, place, sorted } from '../fixtures/legs'

const commuter = pilot('LEX', 'SDF')
const local = pilot(null, 'SDF') // home = base

describe('resolveStatus: a leg covers now', () => {
  it('is in flight while a flight or jumpseat covers now', () => {
    expect(resolveStatus([flight('SDF', 'ANC', 1, 6)], commuter, 2 * H)).toMatchObject({ kind: 'inFlight', airport: 'SDF' })
    expect(resolveStatus([flight('SDF', 'LEX', 1, 2, null)], commuter, 1.5 * H).kind).toBe('inFlight')
  })

  it('gives ground transport its own status', () => {
    expect(resolveStatus([flight('SDF', 'LOU', 1, 2, 'T1', { ground: true })], commuter, 1.5 * H).kind).toBe('ground')
  })

  it('drives home or to work', () => {
    expect(resolveStatus([drive('SDF', 'LEX', true, 1, 2)], commuter, 1.5 * H).kind).toBe('drivingHome')
    expect(resolveStatus([drive('LEX', 'SDF', false, 1, 2)], commuter, 1.5 * H).kind).toBe('drivingToWork')
  })

  it('reports at base, or is off when the trip starts elsewhere', () => {
    expect(resolveStatus([place('report', 'SDF', 0, 1)], commuter, 0.5 * H)).toMatchObject({ kind: 'base', airport: 'SDF' })
    expect(resolveStatus([place('report', 'MIA', 0, 1)], commuter, 0.5 * H)).toMatchObject({ kind: 'off', airport: 'MIA' })
  })

  it.each(['turn', 'layover', 'base', 'home'] as const)('uses the %s leg', (kind) => {
    expect(resolveStatus([place(kind, 'ANC', 0, 5)], commuter, 1 * H)).toMatchObject({ kind, airport: 'ANC' })
  })

  it('is on duty at the event airport, or at base when the event has none', () => {
    expect(resolveStatus([duty('reserve', 'RSV-A', 'MIA', 0, 12)], commuter, 1 * H)).toMatchObject({ kind: 'reserve', airport: 'MIA' })
    expect(resolveStatus([duty('event', 'Meeting', null, 0, 2)], commuter, 1 * H)).toMatchObject({ kind: 'event', airport: 'SDF' })
  })

  it('prefers a flight over an overlapping layover, and a drive over an overlapping event', () => {
    expect(resolveStatus(sorted([place('layover', 'ANC', 0, 10), flight('ANC', 'FAI', 2, 3, null)]), commuter, 2.5 * H).kind).toBe('inFlight')
    expect(resolveStatus(sorted([duty('event', 'Errands', 'LEX', 0, 5), drive('LEX', 'SDF', false, 1, 2)]), commuter, 1.5 * H).kind).toBe('drivingToWork')
  })
})

describe('resolveStatus: between legs (ported from TripStateResolverGapTests.swift)', () => {
  it('is home with no legs at all', () => {
    expect(resolveStatus([], commuter, 0)).toMatchObject({ kind: 'home', airport: 'LEX' })
  })

  it('end of trip at home → home', () => {
    expect(resolveStatus(sorted([flight('SDF', 'ANC', 0, 5), flight('ANC', 'LEX', 20, 25)]), commuter, 30 * H)).toMatchObject({ kind: 'home', airport: 'LEX' })
  })

  it('end of trip at base, commuter → base', () => {
    expect(resolveStatus([flight('ANC', 'SDF', 0, 5)], commuter, 8 * H)).toMatchObject({ kind: 'base', airport: 'SDF' })
  })

  it('end of trip elsewhere → off in that city, never layover or base', () => {
    expect(resolveStatus([flight('SDF', 'MIA', 0, 3)], commuter, 8 * H)).toMatchObject({ kind: 'off', airport: 'MIA' })
    expect(resolveStatus([flight('SDF', 'MIA', 0, 3)], pilot('LEX', null), 8 * H)).toMatchObject({ kind: 'off', airport: 'MIA' })
  })

  it('end of trip at base when home equals base → home', () => {
    expect(resolveStatus([flight('ANC', 'SDF', 0, 5)], local, 8 * H)).toMatchObject({ kind: 'home', airport: 'SDF' })
  })

  it('compares codes after normalizing them (Crewluve compares case-insensitively)', () => {
    const e = engineFor(
      new RawBuilder(A).pilot('Alex', 'sdf', 'lex').trip('t1', {
        dutyPeriods: [{ start: 0, end: 300, flights: [{ dep: 'anc', arr: 'sdf', out: 30, in: 270 }] }],
      }).build(),
    )
    expect(resolveStatus(e.legs, e.schedule.pilot, at(400)).kind).toBe('base')
  })

  it('a gap inside one trip with no place leg is a turn', () => {
    expect(resolveStatus(sorted([flight('SDF', 'RFD', 0, 1), flight('RFD', 'ANC', 3, 8)]), commuter, 2 * H)).toMatchObject({ kind: 'turn', airport: 'RFD' })
  })

  it('jumpseated home before the next trip → home', () => {
    expect(resolveStatus(sorted([flight('SDF', 'LEX', 0, 1, null), flight('LEX', 'ANC', 7, 12, 'T2')]), commuter, 3 * H).kind).toBe('home')
  })

  it('jumpseated to base before the next trip → base', () => {
    expect(resolveStatus(sorted([flight('LEX', 'SDF', 0, 1, null), flight('SDF', 'ANC', 7, 12, 'T2')]), commuter, 3 * H).kind).toBe('base')
  })

  it('a same-trip turn through home stays a turn', () => {
    expect(resolveStatus(sorted([flight('SDF', 'LEX', 0, 1), flight('LEX', 'ANC', 3, 8)]), commuter, 2 * H).kind).toBe('turn')
  })

  it('a same-trip turn through base stays a turn', () => {
    expect(resolveStatus(sorted([flight('MIA', 'SDF', 0, 2), flight('SDF', 'ANC', 4, 9)]), commuter, 3 * H).kind).toBe('turn')
  })

  it('waiting at base for a jumpseat home → commuting home', () => {
    expect(resolveStatus(sorted([flight('ANC', 'SDF', 0, 5), flight('SDF', 'LEX', 7, 8, null)]), commuter, 6 * H)).toMatchObject({ kind: 'commutingHome', airport: 'SDF' })
  })

  it('between two standalone legs elsewhere → off, not a turn (fixes TripStateResolver.swift:469)', () => {
    expect(resolveStatus(sorted([flight('LEX', 'ATL', 0, 1, null), flight('ATL', 'MIA', 3, 4, null)]), commuter, 2 * H)).toMatchObject({ kind: 'off', airport: 'ATL' })
  })

  it('remembers the previous and next legs', () => {
    const legs = sorted([flight('SDF', 'ANC', 0, 5), flight('ANC', 'SDF', 20, 25)])
    const status = resolveStatus(legs, commuter, 10 * H)
    expect(status.previous?.id).toBe(legs[0].id)
    expect(status.next?.id).toBe(legs[1].id)
  })
})
```
