- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/engine/status.ts`**

```ts
import { arrivalOf, isMove, type Leg } from './legs'
import type { Iata, Pilot } from './types'

export type StatusKind =
  | 'home' | 'commutingHome' | 'drivingHome' | 'drivingToWork' | 'inFlight' | 'ground'
  | 'turn' | 'layover' | 'base' | 'reserve' | 'hotStandby' | 'training' | 'event' | 'off' | 'unknown'

export interface Status {
  kind: StatusKind
  /** Where the pilot is: the departure airport while moving, the arrival of the last leg in a gap. */
  airport: Iata | null
  /** The leg covering now, if any. */
  current: Leg | null
  /** The last leg that ended at or before now. */
  previous: Leg | null
  /** The first leg starting after now. */
  next: Leg | null
}

// When legs overlap: a move beats a drive, a drive beats a place, a place beats a duty event (spec §6.4).
const RANK: Record<Leg['kind'], number> = {
  flight: 0, jumpseat: 0, drive: 1, report: 2, turn: 2, layover: 2, base: 2, home: 2,
  reserve: 3, hotStandby: 3, training: 3, event: 3,
}

/** Crewluve's TripStateResolver, delay-aware and with the off-trip fix. `legs` must be sorted by start. */
export function resolveStatus(legs: Leg[], pilot: Pilot, t: number): Status {
  let current: Leg | null = null
  let previous: Leg | null = null
  let next: Leg | null = null
  for (const leg of legs) {
    if (leg.start <= t && t < leg.end) {
      if (!current || RANK[leg.kind] < RANK[current.kind]) current = leg
    } else if (leg.end <= t) {
      if (!previous || leg.end >= previous.end) previous = leg
    } else if (!next) {
      next = leg
    }
  }

  if (current) return { ...covering(current, pilot), current, previous, next }
  if (!previous) return { kind: 'home', airport: pilot.effHome, current, previous, next }

  const loc = arrivalOf(previous) ?? pilot.base
  const result = (kind: StatusKind): Status => ({ kind, airport: loc, current, previous, next })
  if (previous.tripId && next?.tripId === previous.tripId) return result('turn')
  if (loc != null && loc === pilot.effHome) return result('home')
  if (next?.kind === 'jumpseat' && next.arr === pilot.effHome) return result('commutingHome')
  if (loc != null && loc === pilot.base) return result('base')
  return result('off')
}

function covering(leg: Leg, pilot: Pilot): Pick<Status, 'kind' | 'airport'> {
  if (isMove(leg)) return { kind: leg.ground ? 'ground' : 'inFlight', airport: leg.dep }
  switch (leg.kind) {
    case 'drive':
      return { kind: leg.toHome ? 'drivingHome' : 'drivingToWork', airport: leg.from }
    case 'report':
      return { kind: leg.airport === pilot.base ? 'base' : 'off', airport: leg.airport }
    case 'turn':
    case 'layover':
    case 'base':
    case 'home':
      return { kind: leg.kind, airport: leg.airport }
    default:
      return { kind: leg.kind, airport: leg.airport ?? pilot.base }
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/engine/targets.ts`**

```ts
import { DAY, MINUTE } from '../time/zoned'
import { isHomecomingMove, isMove, schedEndOf, type Leg } from './legs'
import type { Status } from './status'
import type { Iata, Pilot } from './types'

export type ArrivalLabel = 'home' | 'base' | 'city' | 'released'

export interface ArrivalTarget {
  label: ArrivalLabel
  time: number
  schedTime: number
  /** The airport the countdown arrives at (the city in the card subtitle). */
  airport: Iata | null
  /** time − schedTime in minutes; positive means late. */
  delayMin: number
}

const RIDE_WINDOW = 3 * DAY

function toTarget(label: ArrivalLabel, leg: Leg, airport: Iata | null): ArrivalTarget {
  const schedTime = schedEndOf(leg)
  return { label, time: leg.end, schedTime, airport, delayMin: Math.round((leg.end - schedTime) / MINUTE) }
}

/** The trip being flown, else one that ended ≤24 h ago, else one starting ≤24 h from now (spec §6.5 step 3). */
export function currentTripId(status: Status, t: number): string | null {
  if (status.current?.tripId) return status.current.tripId
  if (status.previous?.tripId && t - status.previous.end <= DAY) return status.previous.tripId
  if (status.next?.tripId && status.next.start - t <= DAY) return status.next.tripId
  return null
}

/** What the away countdown counts to (spec §6.5), or null for no countdown. Never call it while the status is Home. */
export function arrivalTarget(legs: Leg[], pilot: Pilot, status: Status, t: number): ArrivalTarget | null {
  const current = status.current
  if (current && (current.kind === 'reserve' || current.kind === 'hotStandby' || current.kind === 'training')) {
    return { label: 'released', time: current.end, schedTime: current.end, airport: current.airport ?? pilot.base, delayMin: 0 }
  }
  const home = pilot.effHome
  if (!home) return null
  if (current && !current.tripId && isHomecomingMove(current, home)) return toTarget('home', current, home)

  const trip = currentTripId(status, t)
  const nextTripStart = legs.reduce(
    (min, l) => (l.tripId && l.tripId !== trip && l.start > t ? Math.min(min, l.start) : min),
    Infinity,
  )
  const ride = (from: number, startBy: number) =>
    legs.find((l) => !l.tripId && isHomecomingMove(l, home) && l.end > t && l.start >= from && l.start < nextTripStart && l.start <= startBy)

  if (!trip) {
    const r = ride(-Infinity, t + RIDE_WINDOW)
    return r ? toTarget('home', r, home) : null
  }

  const tripLegs = legs.filter((l) => l.tripId === trip)
  const finalHome = tripLegs.filter(
    (l) => isHomecomingMove(l, home) && l.end > t && !tripLegs.some((m) => m !== l && m.start >= l.end),
  )
  if (finalHome.length > 0) return toTarget('home', finalHome.reduce((a, b) => (b.end > a.end ? b : a)), home)

  const flights = tripLegs.filter(isMove)
  if (flights.length === 0) return null
  const last = flights.reduce((a, b) => (b.end > a.end ? b : a))
  const r = ride(last.start, last.end + RIDE_WINDOW)
  if (r) return toTarget('home', r, home)
  if (last.end > t) return toTarget(last.arr === pilot.base ? 'base' : 'city', last, last.arr)
  return null
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/engine/home.ts`**

```ts
import { MINUTE } from '../time/zoned'
import { departureOf, isHomecomingMove, type Leg, type MoveLeg } from './legs'
import { resolveStatus } from './status'
import { arrivalTarget, type ArrivalTarget } from './targets'
import type { Iata, Pilot } from './types'

export interface LeaveTarget {
  label: 'leavesHome' | 'leaves'
  time: number
  schedTime: number
  viaDrive: boolean
  /** A commuter whose next trip leaves from somewhere other than home, with no commute logged. */
  commuterUnlogged: boolean
  /** Where the departure happens. */
  airport: Iata | null
  /** time − schedTime in minutes; positive means late. */
  delayMin: number
}

/** The first trip flight (not a standalone jumpseat) departing after t. */
export function nextTripFlight(legs: Leg[], t: number): MoveLeg | null {
  return (legs.find((l) => l.kind === 'flight' && l.start > t) as MoveLeg | undefined) ?? null
}

/** When the pilot next leaves home (spec §6.6). Only meaningful while the status is Home. */
export function leaveTarget(legs: Leg[], pilot: Pilot, t: number): LeaveTarget | null {
  const home = pilot.effHome
  const nf = nextTripFlight(legs, t)
  const candidate = legs.find(
    (l) =>
      l.start > t &&
      (!nf || l.start < nf.start) &&
      ((l.kind === 'drive' && !l.toHome) ||
        (l.kind === 'jumpseat' && l.dep === home) ||
        ((l.kind === 'hotStandby' || l.kind === 'training' || l.kind === 'event') && l.airport != null && l.airport === pilot.base)),
  )
  if (candidate) {
    const viaDrive = candidate.kind === 'drive'
    const departsHome = viaDrive || (candidate.kind === 'jumpseat' && candidate.dep === home)
    return {
      label: departsHome ? 'leavesHome' : 'leaves',
      time: candidate.start,
      schedTime: candidate.start,
      viaDrive,
      commuterUnlogged: false,
      airport: departureOf(candidate),
      delayMin: 0,
    }
  }
  if (!nf) return null
  return {
    label: nf.dep === home ? 'leavesHome' : 'leaves',
    time: nf.start,
    schedTime: nf.schedStart,
    viaDrive: false,
    commuterUnlogged: home != null && nf.dep !== home,
    airport: nf.dep,
    delayMin: Math.round((nf.start - nf.schedStart) / MINUTE),
  }
}

/** {HB}: when the next trip brings the pilot home, if that is known (spec §6.6 step 8). */
export function nextTripHomecoming(legs: Leg[], pilot: Pilot, t: number): ArrivalTarget | null {
  const nf = nextTripFlight(legs, t)
  if (!nf) return null
  const target = arrivalTarget(legs, pilot, resolveStatus(legs, pilot, nf.start), nf.start)
  return target?.label === 'home' ? target : null
}

/** End of the last real arrival home at or before t (spec §6.8), skipping passes through home mid-trip. */
export function lastHomeArrival(legs: Leg[], pilot: Pilot, t: number): number | null {
  let best: number | null = null
  for (const l of legs) {
    if (l.end > t || !isHomecomingMove(l, pilot.effHome)) continue
    if (l.tripId && legs.some((m) => m !== l && m.tripId === l.tripId && m.start >= l.end)) continue
    if (best == null || l.end > best) best = l.end
  }
  return best
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { demoRaw } from '../../src/data/demo'
import { RawBuilder } from '../../src/data/rowBuilder'
import { buildLegs, type Leg } from '../../src/engine/legs'
import { buildSchedule } from '../../src/engine/normalize'
import { makeSnapshot, safeSnapshot } from '../../src/engine/snapshot'
import { A, at, engineFor } from '../fixtures/engine'

const NY = 'America/New_York'
const e = engineFor(demoRaw('driver', A))
const snap = (minutes: number) => makeSnapshot(e.schedule, e.legs, at(minutes), NY)

describe('makeSnapshot on the demo timeline', () => {
  it('rings the current flight, report window, layover, or duty — never a turn', () => {
    expect(snap(100).ring).toBeCloseTo(40 / 90)
    expect(snap(30).ring).toBeCloseTo(0.5)
    expect(snap(180).ring).toBeNull()
    expect(snap(4500).ring).toBeCloseTo(0.25)
  })

  it('flags a 15+ minute delay on the flight that matters now', () => {
    expect(snap(1700).delay?.minutes).toBe(40) // in the air on the late leg
    expect(snap(1300).delay?.minutes).toBe(40) // on the layover before it
    expect(snap(100).delay).toBeNull()
  })

  it('celebrates inside the last 24 hours away', () => {
    expect(snap(1700).celebrate).toBe(true)
    expect(snap(100).celebrate).toBe(false)
    expect(snap(30).celebrate).toBe(false) // at base
  })

  it('fills in the home details', () => {
    const s = snap(-1440)
    expect(s.status.kind).toBe('home')
    expect(s.arrival).toBeNull()
    expect(s.daysHome).toBe(3)
    expect(s.lastTripDays).toBe(2)
    expect(s.nextTripDays).toBe(2)
    expect(s.driveToWork?.id).toBe('demo-c1-work')
    expect(s.leave?.time).toBe(at(-90))
    expect(s.homecoming?.time).toBe(at(2000))
    expect(s.nextFlight?.dep).toBe('SDF')
  })

  it('names the pilot', () => {
    expect(snap(0).name).toBe('Alex')
    expect(snap(0).hasName).toBe(true)
  })
})

describe('makeSnapshot special cases', () => {
  it('says "Your pilot" when the profile has no first name', () => {
    const s = buildSchedule(new RawBuilder(A).pilot(null, 'SDF', null).build(), A)
    const sn = makeSnapshot(s, buildLegs(s), A, NY)
    expect(sn.name).toBe('Your pilot')
    expect(sn.hasName).toBe(false)
  })

  it('treats a mid-trip night at home as home, without the been-home extras', () => {
    const s = buildSchedule(
      new RawBuilder(A).pilot('Sam', 'SDF', null).trip('t1', {
        days: 2,
        dutyPeriods: [
          { start: 0, end: 300, flights: [{ dep: 'SDF', arr: 'RFD', out: 60, in: 150 }, { dep: 'RFD', arr: 'SDF', out: 200, in: 290 }] },
          { start: 1300, end: 1700, flights: [{ dep: 'SDF', arr: 'ANC', out: 1360, in: 1660 }] },
        ],
      }).build(),
      A,
    )
    const sn = makeSnapshot(s, buildLegs(s), at(600), NY)
    expect(sn.status.kind).toBe('home')
    expect(sn.midTripAtHome).toBe(true)
    expect(sn.daysHome).toBeNull()
    expect(sn.lastTripDays).toBeNull()
    expect(sn.leave?.time).toBe(at(1360))
  })

  it('shows "On duty" and ~N days for a trip whose flights are all hidden', () => {
    const s = buildSchedule(
      new RawBuilder(A).pilot('Sam', 'SDF', null).trip('t1', {
        days: 3,
        dutyPeriods: [
          { start: 0, end: 600, flights: [{ dep: 'SDF', arr: 'ANC', out: 60, in: 360, hidden: true }] },
          { start: 2880, end: 3300, flights: [{ dep: 'ANC', arr: 'SDF', out: 2940, in: 3240, hidden: true }] },
        ],
      }).build(),
      A,
    )
    const sn = makeSnapshot(s, buildLegs(s), at(1800), NY) // day 2 of 3
    expect(sn.status.kind).toBe('unknown')
    expect(sn.arrival).toBeNull()
    expect(sn.fallbackDaysRemaining).toBe(1)
  })

  it('safeSnapshot turns an engine crash into the "On duty" status', () => {
    const error = vi.spyOn(console, 'error').mockImplementation(() => {})
    const sn = safeSnapshot(e.schedule, null as unknown as Leg[], A, NY)
    expect(sn.status.kind).toBe('unknown')
    expect(sn.arrival).toBeNull()
    expect(error).toHaveBeenCalled()
    error.mockRestore()
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/engine/snapshot.ts`**

```ts
import { calendarDay, DAY, MINUTE } from '../time/zoned'
import { lastHomeArrival, leaveTarget, nextTripFlight, nextTripHomecoming, type LeaveTarget } from './home'
import { arrivalOf, isDuty, isMove, type DriveLeg, type DutyLeg, type Leg, type MoveLeg } from './legs'
import { resolveStatus, type Status } from './status'
import { arrivalTarget, type ArrivalTarget } from './targets'
import type { Pilot, Schedule, Trip } from './types'

export const DELAY_THRESHOLD_MIN = 15

export interface Snapshot {
  t: number
  /** First name, or "Your pilot". */
  name: string
  hasName: boolean
  pilot: Pilot
  status: Status
  /** 0…1 progress for the icon ring, or null where Crewluve shows none. */
  ring: number | null
  /** The relevant flight's departure delay, when it is 15+ minutes off schedule. */
  delay: { flight: MoveLeg; minutes: number } | null
  arrival: ArrivalTarget | null
  fallbackDaysRemaining: number | null
  leave: LeaveTarget | null
  /** {HB}: when the next trip brings the pilot home (Home status only). */
  homecoming: ArrivalTarget | null
  driveToWork: DriveLeg | null
  nextFlight: MoveLeg | null
  nextMove: MoveLeg | null
  /** The next drive or event arriving home ("heads home to …" at base). */
  nextHomeTrip: DriveLeg | DutyLeg | null
  lastTripDays: number | null
  nextTripDays: number | null
  midTripAtHome: boolean
  daysHome: number | null
  celebrate: boolean
  empty: boolean
}

/** Trip length in days: trips.days, else the calendar-day count in `timeZone`. */
export function tripDays(trip: Trip, timeZone: string): number {
  return trip.days ?? calendarDay(trip.end, timeZone) - calendarDay(trip.start, timeZone) + 1
}

/** Everything the screen needs at time t. `legs` must come from buildLegs(schedule). */
export function makeSnapshot(schedule: Schedule, legs: Leg[], t: number, baseTz: string): Snapshot {
  const { pilot } = schedule
  const flightless = flightlessTrip(schedule, legs, t)
  let status = resolveStatus(legs, pilot, t)
  // On a trip whose flights are all hidden we can't tell where the pilot is: Crewluve's "On duty".
  if (flightless && status.kind === 'home' && !status.current) status = { ...status, kind: 'unknown', airport: null }

  const atHome = status.kind === 'home'
  const midTripAtHome = atHome && status.current?.kind === 'home'
  const nextFlight = nextTripFlight(legs, t)
  const nextTrip = nextFlight ? schedule.trips.find((tr) => tr.id === nextFlight.tripId) ?? null : null
  const lastTrip = schedule.trips.reduce<Trip | null>((best, tr) => (tr.end <= t && (!best || tr.end > best.end) ? tr : best), null)
  const arrival = atHome ? null : arrivalTarget(legs, pilot, status, t)
  const nextMove = (legs.find((l) => isMove(l) && l.start > t) as MoveLeg | undefined) ?? null
  const nextHomeTrip = pilot.effHome == null ? null
    : ((legs.find((l) => l.start > t && (l.kind === 'drive' || l.kind === 'event') && arrivalOf(l) === pilot.effHome) as DriveLeg | DutyLeg | undefined) ?? null)

  return {
    t,
    name: pilot.firstName ?? 'Your pilot',
    hasName: pilot.firstName != null,
    pilot,
    status,
    ring: ringProgress(status, t),
    delay: relevantDelay(status, nextFlight, nextMove),
    arrival,
    fallbackDaysRemaining: !atHome && !arrival && flightless ? daysRemaining(flightless, t, baseTz) : null,
    leave: atHome ? leaveTarget(legs, pilot, t) : null,
    homecoming: atHome ? nextTripHomecoming(legs, pilot, t) : null,
    driveToWork: atHome ? pendingDriveToWork(legs, t, nextFlight) : null,
    nextFlight,
    nextMove,
    nextHomeTrip,
    lastTripDays: atHome && lastTrip && !midTripAtHome ? tripDays(lastTrip, baseTz) : null,
    nextTripDays: nextTrip ? tripDays(nextTrip, baseTz) : null,
    midTripAtHome,
    daysHome: atHome && !midTripAtHome ? daysSince(lastHomeArrival(legs, pilot, t), t) : null,
    celebrate: arrival != null && status.kind !== 'base' && arrival.time - t > 0 && arrival.time - t <= DAY,
    empty: schedule.empty,
  }
}

/** makeSnapshot that can never take the page down: an engine error shows "On duty" (spec §8). */
export function safeSnapshot(schedule: Schedule, legs: Leg[], t: number, baseTz: string): Snapshot {
  try {
    return makeSnapshot(schedule, legs, t, baseTz)
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Status engine failed; showing "On duty"', err)
    return {
      t,
      name: schedule.pilot.firstName ?? 'Your pilot',
      hasName: schedule.pilot.firstName != null,
      pilot: schedule.pilot,
      status: { kind: 'unknown', airport: null, current: null, previous: null, next: null },
      ring: null, delay: null, arrival: null, fallbackDaysRemaining: null, leave: null, homecoming: null,
      driveToWork: null, nextFlight: null, nextMove: null, nextHomeTrip: null, lastTripDays: null,
      nextTripDays: null, midTripAtHome: false, daysHome: null, celebrate: false, empty: schedule.empty,
    }
  }
}

function ringProgress(status: Status, t: number): number | null {
  const c = status.current
  if (!c) return null
  const placeRing = (status.kind === 'layover' || status.kind === 'base') && (c.kind === 'layover' || c.kind === 'base' || c.kind === 'report')
  if (!isMove(c) && !isDuty(c) && !placeRing) return null
  return Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (t - c.start) / (c.end - c.start)))
}

function relevantDelay(status: Status, nextFlight: MoveLeg | null, nextMove: MoveLeg | null): Snapshot['delay'] {
  const c = status.current
  const flight =
    c && isMove(c) ? c
      : status.kind === 'home' ? nextFlight
        : status.kind === 'turn' || status.kind === 'layover' || status.kind === 'base' || status.kind === 'off' ? nextMove
          : null
  if (!flight) return null
  const minutes = Math.round((flight.start - flight.schedStart) / MINUTE)
  return Math.abs(minutes) >= DELAY_THRESHOLD_MIN ? { flight, minutes } : null
}

function flightlessTrip(schedule: Schedule, legs: Leg[], t: number): Trip | null {
  const trip = schedule.trips.find((tr) => tr.start <= t && t < tr.end)
  return trip && !legs.some((l) => l.tripId === trip.id && isMove(l)) ? trip : null
}

/** EstimatedReturnCardView: max(total days − day number, 0), counting days in the base's zone. */
function daysRemaining(trip: Trip, t: number, timeZone: string): number {
  const total = tripDays(trip, timeZone)
  const dayNumber = Math.min(calendarDay(t, timeZone) - calendarDay(trip.start, timeZone) + 1, total)
  return Math.max(total - dayNumber, 0)
}

function pendingDriveToWork(legs: Leg[], t: number, nextFlight: MoveLeg | null): DriveLeg | null {
  return (legs.find((l) => l.kind === 'drive' && !l.toHome && l.start > t && (!nextFlight || l.start < nextFlight.start)) as DriveLeg | undefined) ?? null
}

const daysSince = (arrival: number | null, t: number) => (arrival == null ? null : Math.floor((t - arrival) / DAY))
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { narrative, safeNarrative, type NarrativeCtx, type Span } from '../../src/copy/narrative'
import { demoRaw } from '../../src/data/demo'
import { RawBuilder, type FlightSpec } from '../../src/data/rowBuilder'
import type { Leg } from '../../src/engine/legs'
import { makeSnapshot, safeSnapshot } from '../../src/engine/snapshot'
import { testAirports } from '../fixtures/airports'
import { A, at, engineFor } from '../fixtures/engine'

const NY = 'America/New_York'
const ctx = (minutes: number): NarrativeCtx => ({ now: at(minutes), viewerTz: NY, h24: false, airports: testAirports })
const plain = (spans: Span[]) => spans.map((s) => s.text).join('')
const sentenceAt = (e: ReturnType<typeof engineFor>, minutes: number) =>
  narrative(makeSnapshot(e.schedule, e.legs, at(minutes), NY), ctx(minutes))

describe('narrative on the demo timeline', () => {
  const driver = engineFor(demoRaw('driver', A))

  it.each([
    [100, 'Alex is flying to Rockford on UPS flight 2961 from Louisville — landing in 50m which is 8:00am EDT.'],
    [180, 'Alex is in Rockford — heading to Anchorage in 45m which is 9:15am EDT.'],
    [30, 'Alex is at base in Louisville — flies to Rockford in 30m which is 6:30am EDT.'],
    [-7800, 'Alex is on layover in Miami — flies to Louisville in 10h 0m which is 5:30am EDT.'],
    [1300, "Alex's layover has been extended by 40 minutes and will now fly to Louisville in 5h 0m which is 8:10am EDT."],
    [1700, 'Alex was delayed in Anchorage and is flying to Louisville on UPS flight 2962 — landing in 3h 20m which is 1:10pm EDT.'],
    [-60, 'Alex is driving to work in Louisville — arrives in 40m at 5:10am EDT, then the trip starts.'],
    [1950, 'Alex is driving home to Lexington — back in 50m at 2:50pm EDT.'],
    [4500, 'Alex is on reserve in Louisville — on call until 9h 0m which is 5:30pm EDT.'],
    [5900, 'Alex: CQ Training in Louisville — finishes in 5h 40m which is 1:30pm EDT.'],
  ])('at %i min: %s', (minutes, text) => {

  it('jumpseat commuter waiting at base for the ride home', () => {

    )

  it('bolds places and times, and colors the countdown with the status color', () => {
    const spans = sentenceAt(driver, 100)

    expect(spans.filter((s) => s.color).map((s) => `${s.text}:${s.color}`)).toEqual(['50m:status'])
    expect(spans.find((s) => s.text === '50m')?.digits).toBe(true)
  })

    expect(colored(1300)).toEqual(['5h 0m:red'])
    expect(colored(1700)).toEqual(['3h 20m:red'])
    expect(colored(1950)).toEqual(['50m:green'])
  })
})

    const withNext = engineFor(

    )
    expect(plain(sentenceAt(withNext, 600))).toBe('Alex is in Miami — flies to Louisville in 15h 0m which is 6:30am EDT.')
    expect(plain(sentenceAt(engineFor(builder().build()), 600))).toBe('Alex is in Miami')
  })

  it('drops the city for an event with no location and no base', () => {
    const e = engineFor(new RawBuilder(A).pilot('Alex', null, 'LEX').manualEvent('e1', 'Vacation', 0, 600, null).build())
    expect(plain(sentenceAt(e, 60))).toBe('Alex: Vacation — finishes in 9h 0m which is 3:30pm EDT.')
  })

  it('says "On duty" for the unknown status, and when building the sentence fails', () => {
    const error = vi.spyOn(console, 'error').mockImplementation(() => {})
    const driver = engineFor(demoRaw('driver', A))
    expect(plain(narrative(safeSnapshot(driver.schedule, null as unknown as Leg[], A, NY), ctx(0)))).toBe('On duty')
    const broken = {
      ...makeSnapshot(driver.schedule, driver.legs, at(100), NY),
      status: { kind: 'inFlight', airport: 'SDF', current: null, previous: null, next: null },
    } as never
    expect(plain(safeNarrative(broken, ctx(100)))).toBe('On duty')
    error.mockRestore()
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/copy/narrative.ts`**

The `case 'home'` branch calls `homeSentence`. Task 16 fills in the full version; for now it is a one-line stub, so this task compiles and its tests pass.

```ts
import type { AirportIndex } from '../data/airports'
import { arrivalOf, type DriveLeg, type DutyLeg, type MoveLeg } from '../engine/legs'
import type { Snapshot } from '../engine/snapshot'
import { flightPhrase } from './airlines'
import { delayText, narrativeCountdown, timeWithZone } from './format'

export type SpanColor = 'status' | 'green' | 'red'

export interface Span {
  text: string
  bold?: boolean
  /** 'status' means the status color from statusVisual(). */
  color?: SpanColor
  /** Countdown digits: tabular figures and a roll-in when they change. */
  digits?: boolean
}

export interface NarrativeCtx {
  now: number
  viewerTz: string
  h24: boolean
  airports: AirportIndex
}

/** Builds one sentence out of plain, bold, and countdown spans. */
export class Sentence {
  readonly spans: Span[] = []
  constructor(readonly ctx: NarrativeCtx) {}

  t(text: string): this {
    this.spans.push({ text })
    return this
  }

  b(text: string): this {
    this.spans.push({ text, bold: true })
    return this
  }

  cd(target: number, color: SpanColor = 'status'): this {
    this.spans.push({ text: narrativeCountdown(target - this.ctx.now), bold: true, color, digits: true })
    return this
  }

  time(ms: number): this {
    return this.b(timeWithZone(ms, this.ctx.viewerTz, this.ctx.h24))
  }
}

/** Crewluve's narrative card sentence for this snapshot (NarrativeCardView.swift; spec §4.3). */
export function narrative(s: Snapshot, ctx: NarrativeCtx): Span[] {
  const n = new Sentence(ctx)
  const city = (code: string) => ctx.airports.city(code) ?? code
  const name = s.name
  const here = s.status.airport ? city(s.status.airport) : null
  const next = s.nextMove
  const delay = s.delay
  const early = delay != null && delay.minutes < 0
  const changed: SpanColor = early ? 'green' : 'red'

  switch (s.status.kind) {
    case 'home':
      return homeSentence(s, n, city)

    case 'commutingHome':
      if (!next) return n.t(`${name} is heading home`).spans
      return n.t(`${name} has one more leg home from `).b(city(next.dep)).t(' to ').b(city(next.arr))
        .t('! It leaves in ').cd(next.start).t(' at ').time(next.start).t(' and lands at ').time(next.end).t('.').spans

    case 'drivingHome': {
      const d = s.status.current as DriveLeg
      return n.t(`${name} is driving home to `).b(city(d.to)).t(' — back in ').cd(d.end, 'green').t(' at ').time(d.end).t('.').spans
    }

    case 'drivingToWork': {
      const d = s.status.current as DriveLeg
      return n.t(`${name} is driving to work in `).b(city(d.to)).t(' — arrives in ').cd(d.end).t(' at ').time(d.end)
        .t(', then the trip starts.').spans
    }

    case 'ground': {
      const g = s.status.current as MoveLeg
      return n.t(`${name} is riding to `).b(city(g.arr)).t(' by ground from ').b(city(g.dep)).t(' — arrives in ').cd(g.end)
        .t(' which is ').time(g.end).t('.').spans
    }

    case 'inFlight': {
      const f = s.status.current as MoveLeg
      const verb = f.deadhead ? 'riding' : 'flying'
      const fl = flightPhrase(f.airline, f.number)
      if (delay) {
        return n.t(`${name} ${early ? 'pushed early' : 'was delayed'} in `).b(city(f.dep)).t(` and is ${verb} to `).b(city(f.arr))
          .t(`${fl} — landing in `).cd(f.end, changed).t(' which is ').time(f.end).t('.').spans
      }
      return n.t(`${name} is ${verb} to `).b(city(f.arr)).t(`${fl} from `).b(city(f.dep)).t(' — landing in ').cd(f.end)
        .t(' which is ').time(f.end).t('.').spans
    }

    case 'turn':
      if (!here) return n.t(`${name} is between flights`).spans
      if (!next) return n.t(`${name} is in `).b(here).t(' between flights').spans
      if (delay) {
        return n.t(`${name} ${early ? 'is pushing early' : 'is delayed'} in `).b(here).t(' — heading to ').b(city(next.arr))
          .t(' in ').cd(next.start, changed).t(' which is ').time(next.start).t('.').spans
      }
      return n.t(`${name} is in `).b(here).t(' — heading to ').b(city(next.arr)).t(' in ').cd(next.start)
        .t(' which is ').time(next.start).t('.').spans

    case 'layover':
      if (next && delay) {
        return n.t(`${name}'s layover has been ${early ? 'shortened' : 'extended'} by `).b(delayText(delay.minutes))
          .t(' and will now fly to ').b(city(next.arr)).t(' in ').cd(next.start, changed).t(' which is ').time(next.start).t('.').spans
      }
      if (!here) return n.t(`${name} is on layover`).spans
      if (!next) return n.t(`${name} is on layover in `).b(here).spans
      return n.t(`${name} is on layover in `).b(here).t(' — flies to ').b(city(next.arr)).t(' in ').cd(next.start)
        .t(' which is ').time(next.start).t('.').spans

    case 'base': {
      const goHome = s.nextHomeTrip
      const goHomeTo = goHome ? arrivalOf(goHome) : null
      if (here && goHome && goHomeTo && (!next || goHome.start < next.start)) {
        return n.t(`${name} is at base in `).b(here).t(' — heads home to ').b(city(goHomeTo)).t(' in ').cd(goHome.start)
          .t(' which is ').time(goHome.start).t('.').spans
      }
      if (next && delay) {
        return n.t(`${name} ${early ? 'is pushing early' : 'is delayed'} in `).b(city(next.dep))
          .t(` and will be ${next.deadhead ? 'riding' : 'flying'} to `).b(city(next.arr))
          .t(`${flightPhrase(next.airline, next.number)} — departing in `).cd(next.start, changed).t('.').spans
      }
      if (!here) return n.t(`${name} is at base`).spans
      if (!next) return n.t(`${name} is at base in `).b(here).spans
      return n.t(`${name} is at base in `).b(here).t(' — flies to ').b(city(next.arr)).t(' in ').cd(next.start)
        .t(' which is ').time(next.start).t('.').spans
    }

    case 'reserve':
    case 'hotStandby': {
      const d = s.status.current as DutyLeg
      const what = s.status.kind === 'reserve' ? 'on reserve' : 'on hot standby'
      if (!here) return n.t(`${name} is ${what}`).spans
      return n.t(`${name} is ${what} in `).b(here).t(' — on call until ').cd(d.end).t(' which is ').time(d.end).t('.').spans
    }

    case 'training':
    case 'event': {
      const d = s.status.current as DutyLeg
      n.t(`${name}: `).b(d.title)
      if (here) n.t(' in ').b(here)
      return n.t(' — finishes in ').cd(d.end).t(' which is ').time(d.end).t('.').spans
    }

    case 'off':
      if (!here) return n.t(`${name} is off duty`).spans
      if (!next) return n.t(`${name} is in `).b(here).spans
      return n.t(`${name} is in `).b(here).t(' — flies to ').b(city(next.arr)).t(' in ').cd(next.start)
        .t(' which is ').time(next.start).t('.').spans

    default:
      return [{ text: 'On duty' }]
  }
}

/** narrative() that can never take the page down. */
export function safeNarrative(s: Snapshot, ctx: NarrativeCtx): Span[] {
  try {
    return narrative(s, ctx)
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Narrative failed; showing "On duty"', err)
    return [{ text: 'On duty' }]
  }
}

// Replaced with the full Home templates in Task 16.
function homeSentence(s: Snapshot, n: Sentence, _city: (code: string) => string) {
  return n.t(`${s.name} is home and relaxing.`).spans
}
```

`noUnusedParameters` flags `_city` on this stub. TypeScript skips parameters whose names start with an underscore, so the build stays clean. Task 16 replaces the stub.

```ts
// Replaced with the full Home templates in Task 16.
```

with:

```ts
/** Home templates (NarrativeCardView.swift:106-171). */
function homeSentence(s: Snapshot, n: Sentence, city: (code: string) => string): Span[] {
  const { name } = s
  const nf = s.nextFlight
  const { now, viewerTz, h24 } = n.ctx

  // Commuter at home with the drive to work logged: tell the work story (no delay suffix, as in the app).
  if (s.driveToWork && nf) {
    return n.t(`${name} is home — heads to base, then on to `).b(city(nf.arr)).t(', departing in ').cd(nf.start)
      .t(' at ').time(nf.start).t('.').spans
  }

  const prev = s.lastTripDays
  const hb = s.homecoming
  // {HB}: ". Back home in {cd} on **Monday October 12th at 8:02pm EDT**."
  const backHome = (homecoming: number) =>
    n.t('. Back home in ').cd(homecoming, 'green').t(' on ').b(longDateTimeText(homecoming, viewerTz, h24)).t('.')

  if (nf) {
    const dep = departureText(nf.start, now, viewerTz, h24)
    const dest = city(nf.arr)
    const commute = s.pilot.effHome && nf.dep !== s.pilot.effHome ? city(nf.dep) : null
    const goneFor = daysText(s.nextTripDays ?? 1)
    if (prev != null) {
      n.t(`${name} is home after being gone for `).b(daysText(prev)).t('! ')
      if (commute) n.t('Commuting to ').b(commute).t(' then heading to ').b(dest).t(` ${dep}`)
      else n.t('Heading to ').b(dest).t(` ${dep}`)
      if (hb) backHome(hb.time)
      else n.t(' — gone for ').b(goneFor).t('.')
    } else {
      n.t(`${name} is home — `)
      if (commute) n.t('commutes to ').b(commute).t(' then heads to ').b(dest).t(` ${dep}`)
      else n.t('heads to ').b(dest).t(` ${dep}`)
      if (hb) backHome(hb.time)
      else n.t(' and will be gone for ').b(goneFor).t('.')
    }
  } else if (prev != null) {
    n.t(`${name} is home after being gone for `).b(daysText(prev)).t(' and is relaxing.')
  } else {
    n.t(`${name} is home and relaxing.`)
  }

  const delay = s.delay
  if (delay && delay.minutes >= DELAY_THRESHOLD_MIN) {
    n.t(' The flight to ').b(city(delay.flight.arr)).t(' is delayed ').b(delayText(delay.minutes)).t('.')
  }
  return n.spans
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/copy/visuals.ts`**

```ts
import type { AirportIndex } from '../data/airports'
import type { LeaveTarget } from '../engine/home'
import type { DutyLeg, MoveLeg } from '../engine/legs'
import { DELAY_THRESHOLD_MIN, type Snapshot } from '../engine/snapshot'
import type { ArrivalTarget } from '../engine/targets'
import type { IconName } from '../icons/iconPaths'
import { airlineColor, airlineIcon } from './airlines'
import type { ColorToken } from './colors'

export interface Visual {
  icon: IconName
  color: ColorToken
}

export interface CardVisual extends Visual {
  title: string
}

const DRIVE_TITLE = /driv|truck|car/i

/** Status icon and color (NarrativeCardView.swift:772-806). */
export function statusVisual(s: Snapshot): Visual {
  const late = s.delay != null && s.delay.minutes > 0
  const early = s.delay != null && s.delay.minutes < 0
  switch (s.status.kind) {
    case 'home':
      return { icon: 'House', color: 'green' }
    case 'commutingHome':
      return { icon: 'Airplane', color: 'blue' }
    case 'drivingHome':
      return { icon: 'Car', color: 'green' }
    case 'drivingToWork':
    case 'ground':
      return { icon: 'Car', color: 'blue' }
    case 'inFlight': {
      const f = s.status.current as MoveLeg
      return { icon: airlineIcon(f.airline), color: early ? 'green' : late ? 'orange' : airlineColor(f.airline) }
    }
    case 'turn':
      return { icon: 'ArrowsClockwise', color: early ? 'green' : 'orange' }
    case 'layover':
      return { icon: 'Bed', color: early ? 'green' : late ? 'orange' : 'purple' }
    case 'base':
      return { icon: 'Buildings', color: 'purple' }
    case 'reserve':
      return { icon: 'ClockCountdown', color: 'red' }
    case 'hotStandby':
      return { icon: 'Lightning', color: 'orange' }
    case 'training':
    case 'event': {
      const title = (s.status.current as DutyLeg | null)?.title ?? ''
      return { icon: DRIVE_TITLE.test(title) ? 'Car' : 'BookOpen', color: 'purple' }
    }
    case 'off':
      return { icon: 'MapPin', color: 'secondary' }
    default:
      return { icon: 'Circle', color: 'gray' }
  }
}

/** The away countdown card (PilotStatusView.swift:133-150, plus the web-only "Released In"). */
export function arrivalCard(s: Snapshot, a: ArrivalTarget, airports: AirportIndex): CardVisual {
  if (a.label === 'released') return { title: 'Released In', ...statusVisual(s) }
  const title =
    a.label === 'home' ? 'Back Home In'
      : a.label === 'base' ? 'Back at Base In'
        : `${airports.city(a.airport) ?? 'Trip End'} In`
  return { title, icon: a.label === 'home' ? 'House' : 'AirplaneLanding', color: a.delayMin >= DELAY_THRESHOLD_MIN ? 'red' : 'green' }
}

/** The at-home countdown card (PilotStatusView.swift:102-114, 306-317). */
export function leaveCard(l: LeaveTarget): CardVisual {
  const color: ColorToken =
    l.viaDrive ? 'blue'
      : l.delayMin >= DELAY_THRESHOLD_MIN ? 'orange'
        : l.delayMin <= -DELAY_THRESHOLD_MIN ? 'green'
          : 'blue'
  return { title: l.label === 'leavesHome' ? 'Leaves Home In' : 'Leaves In', icon: l.viaDrive ? 'Car' : 'AirplaneTakeoff', color }
}
```

### Task 18: Supabase client, fetchSchedule, and the read-only guard

**Files:**
- Create: `src/cloud.ts`, `src/supabase.ts`, `src/data/fetchSchedule.ts`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing fetch test `tests/data/fetchSchedule.test.ts`**

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { fetchSchedule, ScheduleFetchError, WINDOW_FUTURE, WINDOW_PAST, type SelectClient } from '../../src/data/fetchSchedule'
import { COLUMNS } from '../../src/data/rows'

type Call = [string, ...unknown[]]
interface Failure { table: string; message: string; code: string; status: number }

class FakeQuery {
  readonly calls: Call[] = []
  constructor(readonly table: string, private readonly pages: unknown[][], private readonly failure: Failure | null) {}
  select(columns: string) { this.calls.push(['select', columns]); return this }
  is(column: string, value: unknown) { this.calls.push(['is', column, value]); return this }
  gte(column: string, value: string) { this.calls.push(['gte', column, value]); return this }
  lte(column: string, value: string) { this.calls.push(['lte', column, value]); return this }
  order(column: string, options: unknown) { this.calls.push(['order', column, options]); return this }
  async range(from: number, to: number) {
    this.calls.push(['range', from, to])
    if (this.failure) return { data: null, error: { message: this.failure.message, code: this.failure.code }, status: this.failure.status }
    return { data: this.pages[from / 1000] ?? [], error: null, status: 200 }
  }
}

function fakeClient(pages: Record<string, unknown[][]> = {}, failure: Failure | null = null) {
  const queries: FakeQuery[] = []
  const client: SelectClient = {
    from(table: string) {
      const q = new FakeQuery(table, pages[table] ?? [[]], failure?.table === table ? failure : null)
      queries.push(q)
      return q
    },
  }
  return { client, queries }
}

const NOW = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 12)
const iso = (ms: number) => new Date(ms).toISOString()
const row = (id: string, updatedAt = '2026-10-01T00:00:00.000Z') => ({ id, updated_at: updatedAt })

describe('fetchSchedule', () => {
  it('reads exactly the nine schedule tables, with their columns, live rows only, ordered by id', async () => {
    const { client, queries } = fakeClient()
    await fetchSchedule(client, NOW)
    expect(new Set(queries.map((q) => q.table))).toEqual(new Set(Object.keys(COLUMNS)))
    for (const q of queries) {
      expect(q.calls[0]).toEqual(['select', COLUMNS[q.table as keyof typeof COLUMNS]])
      expect(q.calls).toContainEqual(['is', 'deleted_at', null])
      expect(q.calls).toContainEqual(['order', 'id', { ascending: true }])
    }
  })

  it('limits schedule tables to 60 days back and 120 days ahead', async () => {
    const { client, queries } = fakeClient()
    await fetchSchedule(client, NOW)
    const calls = (table: string) => queries.find((q) => q.table === table)!.calls
    expect(calls('flights')).toContainEqual(['gte', 'scheduled_block_out_time', iso(NOW - WINDOW_PAST)])
    expect(calls('flights')).toContainEqual(['lte', 'scheduled_block_out_time', iso(NOW + WINDOW_FUTURE)])
    expect(calls('trips')).toContainEqual(['gte', 'end_date', iso(NOW - WINDOW_PAST)])
    expect(calls('trips')).toContainEqual(['lte', 'start_date', iso(NOW + WINDOW_FUTURE)])
  })

  it('pages through more than 1000 rows', async () => {
    const full = Array.from({ length: 1000 }, (_, i) => row(`f${i}`))
    const { client, queries } = fakeClient({ flights: [full, [row('f1000'), row('f1001')]] })
    const raw = await fetchSchedule(client, NOW)
    expect(raw.flights).toHaveLength(1002)
    const ranges = queries.filter((q) => q.table === 'flights').map((q) => q.calls.find((c) => c[0] === 'range'))
    expect(ranges).toEqual([['range', 0, 999], ['range', 1000, 1999]])
  })

  it('stamps the fetch time and the newest update', async () => {
    const { client } = fakeClient({ trips: [[row('t1', '2026-10-05T08:00:00.000Z')]], flights: [[row('f1', '2026-10-06T09:00:00.000Z')]] })
    const raw = await fetchSchedule(client, NOW)
    expect(raw.fetchedAt).toBe(NOW)
    expect(raw.newestUpdatedAt).toBe(Date.parse('2026-10-06T09:00:00.000Z'))
  })

  it('fails the whole fetch on any table error, flagging expired sessions', async () => {
    const { client } = fakeClient({}, { table: 'flights', message: 'JWT expired', code: 'PGRST301', status: 401 })
    const err = await fetchSchedule(client, NOW).catch((e: unknown) => e)
    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(ScheduleFetchError)
    expect((err as ScheduleFetchError).isAuth).toBe(true)
  })
})
```

```ts
import { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from 'node:fs'
import { basename, join } from 'node:path'
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'

function sourceFiles(dir: string): string[] {
  return readdirSync(dir).flatMap((name) => {
    const path = join(dir, name)
    if (statSync(path).isDirectory()) return sourceFiles(path)
    return /\.(ts|tsx)$/.test(name) ? [path] : []
  })
}

// Resolved from this file, so the guard scans src/ whatever directory the tests run from.
const files = sourceFiles(fileURLToPath(new URL('../../src', import.meta.url)))

describe('read-only guarantee (spec §9)', () => {
  it.each(files)('%s never writes to CrewLu Cloud or injects HTML', (file) => {
    const code = readFileSync(file, 'utf8')
    expect(code).not.toMatch(/\.(insert|update|upsert|delete|rpc)\s*\(/)
    expect(code).not.toMatch(/\.storage\b/)
    expect(code).not.toMatch(/innerHTML|dangerouslySetInnerHTML/)
  })

  it('keeps all database access in fetchSchedule.ts', () => {
    const callers = files.filter((f) => /\b(client|supabase)\.from\(/.test(readFileSync(f, 'utf8')))
    expect(callers.map((f) => basename(f))).toEqual(['fetchSchedule.ts'])
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/data/fetchSchedule.ts`**

```ts
import { DAY } from '../time/zoned'
import {
  COLUMNS, newestUpdatedAt,
  type CommuteRow, type DutyPeriodRow, type EventCategoryRow, type FlightRow, type JumpseatRow,
  type ManualEventRow, type PilotInfoRow, type RawSchedule, type TrainingEventRow, type TripRow,
} from './rows'

export const WINDOW_PAST = 60 * DAY
export const WINDOW_FUTURE = 120 * DAY
const PAGE = 1000

/** The slice of the Supabase client this module uses, so tests can pass a fake. */
export interface SelectClient {
  // PostgREST's builder types are deep generics; this module only chains select/is/gte/lte/order/range.
  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
  from(table: string): any
}

export class ScheduleFetchError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string, readonly status: number | null, readonly code: string | null) {
    super(message)
    this.name = 'ScheduleFetchError'
  }

  /** The session is no longer valid: send the visitor back to sign-in. */
  get isAuth(): boolean {
    return this.status === 401 || this.code === 'PGRST301' || this.code === 'PGRST302'
  }
}

type Table = keyof typeof COLUMNS
// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
type Filter = (query: any) => any

async function selectAll<T>(client: SelectClient, table: Table, filter: Filter): Promise<T[]> {
  const rows: T[] = []
  for (let from = 0; ; from += PAGE) {
    const res = await filter(client.from(table).select(COLUMNS[table]).is('deleted_at', null))
      .order('id', { ascending: true })
      .range(from, from + PAGE - 1)
    if (res.error) throw new ScheduleFetchError(res.error.message ?? `${table} failed`, res.status ?? null, res.error.code ?? null)
    const page = (res.data ?? []) as T[]
    rows.push(...page)
    if (page.length < PAGE) return rows
  }
}

/** Reads the signed-in pilot's schedule window from CrewLu Cloud. Read-only: select queries only (spec §6.1). */
export async function fetchSchedule(client: SelectClient, now: number): Promise<RawSchedule> {
  const from = new Date(now - WINDOW_PAST).toISOString()
  const to = new Date(now + WINDOW_FUTURE).toISOString()
  const everything: Filter = (q) => q
  const [pilotInfos, trips, dutyPeriods, flights, commutes, jumpseats, trainingEvents, manualEvents, eventCategories] = await Promise.all([
    selectAll<PilotInfoRow>(client, 'pilot_infos', everything),
    selectAll<TripRow>(client, 'trips', (q) => q.gte('end_date', from).lte('start_date', to)),
    selectAll<DutyPeriodRow>(client, 'duty_periods', (q) => q.gte('start_time', from).lte('start_time', to)),
    selectAll<FlightRow>(client, 'flights', (q) => q.gte('scheduled_block_out_time', from).lte('scheduled_block_out_time', to)),
    selectAll<CommuteRow>(client, 'commutes', (q) => q.gte('arrival_time_zulu', from).lte('departure_time_zulu', to)),
    selectAll<JumpseatRow>(client, 'jumpseats', (q) => q.gte('arrival_time_zulu', from).lte('departure_time_zulu', to)),
    selectAll<TrainingEventRow>(client, 'training_events', (q) => q.gte('end_time_utc', from).lte('start_time_utc', to)),
    selectAll<ManualEventRow>(client, 'manual_events', (q) => q.gte('end_time_utc', from).lte('start_time_utc', to)),
    selectAll<EventCategoryRow>(client, 'event_categories', everything),
  ])
  const rows = { pilotInfos, trips, dutyPeriods, flights, commutes, jumpseats, trainingEvents, manualEvents, eventCategories }
  return { ...rows, fetchedAt: now, newestUpdatedAt: newestUpdatedAt(rows) }
}
```

The `eslint-disable` comments are only documentation, since the project doesn't run ESLint. The `any` is deliberate and limited to the builder chain.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { CACHE_KEY, localScheduleCache, noScheduleCache, type KeyValueStore } from '../../src/data/cache'
import { RawBuilder } from '../../src/data/rowBuilder'

function memoryStore(initial: Record<string, string> = {}): KeyValueStore & { data: Record<string, string> } {
  const data = { ...initial }
  return {
    data,
    getItem: (key) => data[key] ?? null,
    setItem: (key, value) => { data[key] = value },
    removeItem: (key) => { delete data[key] },
  }
}

    expect(cache.load()).toEqual(raw)

    expect(localScheduleCache(memoryStore({ [CACHE_KEY]: '{"fetchedAt":1}' })).load()).toBeNull()
    expect(localScheduleCache(memoryStore()).load()).toBeNull()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/data/cache.ts`**

```ts
import type { RawSchedule } from './rows'

export const CACHE_KEY = 'crewluve.schedule.v1'

export type KeyValueStore = Pick<Storage, 'getItem' | 'setItem' | 'removeItem'>

export interface ScheduleCache {
  load(): RawSchedule | null
  save(raw: RawSchedule): void
  clear(): void
}

const TABLES = ['pilotInfos', 'trips', 'dutyPeriods', 'flights', 'commutes', 'jumpseats', 'trainingEvents', 'manualEvents', 'eventCategories'] as const

function isRawSchedule(value: unknown): value is RawSchedule {
  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object') return false
  const o = value as Record<string, unknown>
  return typeof o.fetchedAt === 'number' && TABLES.every((t) => Array.isArray(o[t]))
}

function browserStorage(): KeyValueStore | null {
  try {
    return window.localStorage
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

/** The last fetched schedule, kept on this device so the page opens instantly and works offline (spec §5). */
export function localScheduleCache(store: KeyValueStore | null = browserStorage()): ScheduleCache {
  return {
    load() {
      try {
        const text = store?.getItem(CACHE_KEY)
        const value: unknown = text ? JSON.parse(text) : null
        return isRawSchedule(value) ? value : null
      } catch {
        return null
      }
    },
    save(raw) {
      try {
        store?.setItem(CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(raw))
      } catch {
        // Storage full or blocked: carry on without a cache.
      }
    },
    clear() {
      try {
        store?.removeItem(CACHE_KEY)
      } catch {
        // Nothing to clear.
      }
    },
  }
}

/** The demo keeps nothing. */
export const noScheduleCache: ScheduleCache = {
  load: () => null,
  save: () => {},
  clear: () => {},
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/auth/signInErrors.ts`**

```ts
export type SignInError = 'credentials' | 'network' | 'other'

export const SIGN_IN_MESSAGES: Record<SignInError, string> = {
  credentials: "That email and password didn't work. Use the same CrewLu Cloud sign-in as the Crewlu app.",
  network: "Couldn't reach CrewLu Cloud. Check your connection and try again.",
  other: 'Sign-in failed. Try again in a minute.',
}

/** Maps a Supabase auth error (or a thrown fetch error) to one of the three messages in spec §4.2. */
export function classifySignInError(err: { status?: number; name?: string; message?: string } | null | undefined): SignInError {
  const message = (err?.message ?? '').toLowerCase()
  if (err?.status === 400 || message.includes('invalid login credentials')) return 'credentials'
  if (err?.name === 'AuthRetryableFetchError' || err?.status === 0 || message.includes('failed to fetch') || message.includes('network')) {
    return 'network'
  }
  return 'other'
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/auth/session.ts`** (thin wrappers around the Supabase client; the UI tests inject fakes instead)

```ts
import { supabase } from '../supabase'
import { classifySignInError, type SignInError } from './signInErrors'

/** Signs in with the pilot's CrewLu Cloud email and password; null means success. */
export async function signIn(email: string, password: string): Promise<SignInError | null> {
  try {
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password })
    return error ? classifySignInError(error) : null
  } catch (err) {
    return classifySignInError(err as { name?: string; message?: string })
  }
}

export async function hasSession(): Promise<boolean> {
  const { data } = await supabase.auth.getSession()
  return data.session != null
}

/** Calls `onOut` when the session ends (signed out elsewhere, or a refresh failed). Returns an unsubscribe. */
export function onSignedOut(onOut: () => void): () => void {
  const { data } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((event) => {
    if (event === 'SIGNED_OUT') onOut()
  })
  return () => data.subscription.unsubscribe()
}

export async function signOut(): Promise<void> {
  await supabase.auth.signOut()
}
```

```ts
})
```

### Task 22: Brand icons

This builds the favicon, the home-screen icon, and the sign-in heart from the Crewluve app's own artwork, `Crewluv/CrewluveIcon.icon/Assets/Heart icon.png` (1024×1024, transparent). The background copies the app icon's gradient from `icon.json`: blue at the bottom, pink from 30% below the top.

**Files:**
- Create: `scripts/make-icons.py`, `public/icons/apple-touch-icon.png`, `public/icons/icon-192.png`, `public/icons/favicon-32.png`, `public/icons/heart-256.png`
- Modify: `index.html`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write `scripts/make-icons.py`**

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Builds the website icons from the Crewluve app artwork.

Mirrors Crewluv/CrewluveIcon.icon/icon.json: a vertical gradient (blue at the bottom, pink from
30% below the top upward) with the heart artwork on top. Writes PNGs to public/icons/.
Run: python3 -I scripts/make-icons.py
"""
from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image

ART = Path('~/Dev/Crewluv/CrewluveIcon.icon/Assets/Heart icon.png')
OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / 'public' / 'icons'
SIZE = 1024
BLUE = (106, 191, 255)  # icon.json display-p3 (0.417, 0.749, 1.0), approximated in sRGB
PINK = (255, 177, 233)  # icon.json display-p3 (1.0, 0.694, 0.916)


def background() -> Image.Image:
    column = Image.new('RGBA', (1, SIZE))
    for y in range(SIZE):
        f = min(1.0, (1.0 - y / (SIZE - 1)) / 0.7)  # 0 at the bottom edge, 1 from 30% below the top
        column.putpixel((0, y), tuple(round(BLUE[i] + (PINK[i] - BLUE[i]) * f) for i in range(3)) + (255,))
    return column.resize((SIZE, SIZE))


def main() -> None:
    OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    heart = Image.open(ART).convert('RGBA')
    icon = background()
    art = heart.resize((round(SIZE * 0.86), round(SIZE * 0.86)), Image.LANCZOS)
    icon.alpha_composite(art, ((SIZE - art.width) // 2, (SIZE - art.height) // 2))
    for name, size in [('apple-touch-icon.png', 180), ('icon-192.png', 192), ('favicon-32.png', 32)]:
        icon.resize((size, size), Image.LANCZOS).save(OUT / name)
    heart.resize((256, 256), Image.LANCZOS).save(OUT / 'heart-256.png')
    print('wrote icons to', OUT)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/ui/colors.ts`**

```ts
import type { ColorToken } from '../copy/colors'

/** A ColorToken as CSS: semantic names map to the theme variables in src/styles/tokens.css. */
export function cssColor(token: ColorToken): string {
  if (token.startsWith('#')) return token
  return token === 'secondary' ? 'var(--text-2)' : `var(--${token})`
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/ui/Icon.tsx`**

```tsx
import { ICON_PATHS, type IconName } from '../icons/iconPaths'

interface IconProps {
  name: IconName
  size?: number
  /** Any CSS color; defaults to the surrounding text color. */
  color?: string
  /** Accessible name. Omit it for decorative icons. */
  label?: string
}

export function Icon({ name, size = 28, color, label }: IconProps) {
  return (
    <svg
      class="icon"
      width={size}
      height={size}
      viewBox="0 0 256 256"
      fill="currentColor"
      style={color ? { color } : undefined}
      role={label ? 'img' : undefined}
      aria-label={label}
      aria-hidden={label ? undefined : 'true'}
    >
      {ICON_PATHS[name].map((d, i) => (
        <path key={i} d={d} />
      ))}
    </svg>
  )
}
```

```tsx
export function titleFor(name: string | null): string {
  return name ? `Where's ${name}?` : "Where's your pilot?"
}

export function Title({ text }: { text: string }) {
  return <h1 class="title">{text}</h1>
}
```

```tsx
const RADIUS = 20.5 // 44 px box with a 3 px stroke
const CIRCUMFERENCE = 2 * Math.PI * RADIUS

/** NarrativeCardView's ring: 20% track, round-capped fill from 12 o'clock, clockwise. */
export function ProgressRing({ value, color }: { value: number; color: string }) {
  return (
    <svg class="ring" width="44" height="44" viewBox="0 0 44 44" aria-hidden="true">
      <circle cx="22" cy="22" r={RADIUS} fill="none" style={{ stroke: color, strokeOpacity: 0.2, strokeWidth: 3 }} />
      <circle
        cx="22"
        cy="22"
        r={RADIUS}
        fill="none"
        transform="rotate(-90 22 22)"
        style={{ stroke: color, strokeWidth: 3, strokeLinecap: 'round', strokeDasharray: `${CIRCUMFERENCE * value} ${CIRCUMFERENCE}` }}
      />
    </svg>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write `src/ui/NarrativeCard.tsx`**

```tsx
import type { Span } from '../copy/narrative'
import type { Visual } from '../copy/visuals'
import { cssColor } from './colors'
import { Icon } from './Icon'
import { ProgressRing } from './ProgressRing'

/** The "what's happening now" card (NarrativeCardView). */
export function NarrativeCard({ spans, visual, ring }: { spans: Span[]; visual: Visual; ring: number | null }) {
  const statusColor = cssColor(visual.color)
  return (
    <section class="card glass narrative">
      <div class="narrative-icon">
        {ring != null && <ProgressRing value={ring} color={statusColor} />}
        <Icon name={visual.icon} size={28} color={statusColor} />
      </div>
      <p class="narrative-text">
        {spans.map((span, i) => (
          <span
            // Countdown spans are keyed by their text, so a new minute remounts them and replays the roll-in.
            key={span.digits ? `cd-${i}-${span.text}` : i}
            class={[span.bold && 'b', span.digits && 'digits'].filter(Boolean).join(' ') || undefined}
            style={span.color ? { color: span.color === 'status' ? statusColor : cssColor(span.color) } : undefined}
          >
            {span.text}
          </span>
        ))}
      </p>
    </section>
  )
}
```

### Task 25: Celebration

This ports Crewluve `CelebrationFigureView.swift` exactly:
- a 36×28 stick figure whose arms wave every ~0.67 s
- six hearts rising 35 px over 1 s each
- a bounce (scale, offset, and rotation keyframes)
- 2.0 s visible, then a 0.4 s fade

The motion math is pure and unit-tested. The component only paints frames.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/ui/celebrationMath.ts`, `src/ui/Celebration.tsx`
- Test: `tests/ui/celebrationMath.test.ts`, `tests/ui/Celebration.test.tsx`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing math test `tests/ui/celebrationMath.test.ts`**

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { CELEBRATION_MS, celebrationFrame, figureGeometry } from '../../src/ui/celebrationMath'

describe('celebrationFrame', () => {
  it('lasts 2.4 s: 2 s visible, then a 0.4 s fade', () => {
    expect(CELEBRATION_MS).toBe(2400)
    expect(celebrationFrame(-1)).toBeNull()
    expect(celebrationFrame(2400)).toBeNull()
    expect(celebrationFrame(1000)?.opacity).toBe(1)
    expect(celebrationFrame(2200)?.opacity).toBeCloseTo(0.5)
  })

  it('waves the arms about every 0.67 s', () => {
    expect(celebrationFrame(0)?.armRaise).toBeCloseTo(1)
    expect(celebrationFrame(1000 / 3)?.armRaise).toBeCloseTo(0)
  })

  it('releases hearts that rise 35 px over a second while fading and shrinking', () => {
    expect(celebrationFrame(0)?.hearts).toEqual([])
    const frame = celebrationFrame(550)!
    expect(frame.hearts).toHaveLength(3) // released at 0.05, 0.25, and 0.50 s
    const [first] = frame.hearts
    expect(first.x).toBeCloseTo(-8)
    expect(first.y).toBeCloseTo(-29.5)
    expect(first.size).toBe(10)
    expect(first.opacity).toBeCloseTo(0.5)
    expect(first.scale).toBeCloseTo(0.8)
  })

  it('bounces the figure through Crewluve’s keyframes', () => {
    expect(celebrationFrame(0)?.scale).toBe(1)
    expect(celebrationFrame(300)?.scale).toBeCloseTo(1.3)
    expect(celebrationFrame(1400)?.scale).toBeCloseTo(1)
  })
})

describe('figureGeometry', () => {
  it('raises the hands toward the head', () => {
    expect(figureGeometry(0).rHand.x).toBeCloseTo(26.64)
    expect(figureGeometry(0).rHand.y).toBeCloseTo(2.8)
    expect(figureGeometry(1).rHand.x).toBeCloseTo(22.32)
    expect(figureGeometry(1).rHand.y).toBe(0)
    expect(figureGeometry(1).lHand.x).toBeCloseTo(36 - 22.32)

})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/ui/celebrationMath.ts`**

```ts
/** Pure motion math for Crewluve's CelebrationFigureView (spec §4.4). */

export const CELEBRATION_MS = 2400 // 2.0 s visible + 0.4 s fade

export interface HeartFrame {
  x: number
  y: number
  size: number
  opacity: number
  scale: number
}

export interface CelebrationFrame {
  opacity: number
  /** 0 = arms low, 1 = arms raised. */
  armRaise: number
  scale: number
  offsetY: number
  rotation: number
  hearts: HeartFrame[]
}

interface Point {
  x: number
  y: number
}

const HEARTS = [
  { x: -6, delay: 0.05, size: 10, drift: -4 },
  { x: 8, delay: 0.25, size: 8, drift: 5 },
  { x: -2, delay: 0.5, size: 12, drift: -2 },
  { x: 12, delay: 0.75, size: 9, drift: 6 },
  { x: -10, delay: 1.0, size: 7, drift: -6 },
  { x: 4, delay: 1.25, size: 11, drift: 3 },
]

const SCALE_KEYS: [number, number][] = [[1.3, 0.3], [0.9, 0.25], [1.2, 0.3], [1.0, 0.3]]
const OFFSET_KEYS: [number, number][] = [[-12, 0.25], [0, 0.2], [-10, 0.25], [0, 0.2], [-6, 0.2], [0, 0.2]]
const ROTATION_KEYS: [number, number][] = [[8, 0.25], [-8, 0.3], [6, 0.25], [-6, 0.25], [0, 0.25]]

/** Keyframe track: ease out from the previous value to each [value, seconds] in turn (stands in for SpringKeyframe). */
function track(keys: [number, number][], seconds: number, initial: number): number {
  let from = initial
  let start = 0
  for (const [to, duration] of keys) {
    if (seconds < start + duration) {
      const p = (seconds - start) / duration
      return from + (to - from) * (1 - (1 - p) * (1 - p))
    }
    from = to
    start += duration
  }
  return from
}

/** One animation frame, or null once the celebration is over. */
export function celebrationFrame(elapsedMs: number): CelebrationFrame | null {
  if (elapsedMs < 0 || elapsedMs >= CELEBRATION_MS) return null
  const e = elapsedMs / 1000
  const hearts: HeartFrame[] = []
  for (const h of HEARTS) {
    const t = e - h.delay
    if (t > 0 && t < 1) hearts.push({ x: h.x + h.drift * t, y: -t * 35 - 12, size: h.size, opacity: 1 - t, scale: 1 - t * 0.4 })
  }
  return {
    opacity: e < 2 ? 1 : 1 - (e - 2) / 0.4,
    armRaise: Math.cos(e * Math.PI * 3) * 0.5 + 0.5,
    scale: track(SCALE_KEYS, e, 1),
    offsetY: track(OFFSET_KEYS, e, 0),
    rotation: track(ROTATION_KEYS, e, 0),
    hearts,
  }
}

/** The 36×28 stick figure (CelebrationFigureView.drawFigure). */
export function figureGeometry(armRaise: number): {
  headR: number
  headCy: number
  shoulderY: number
  lineWidth: number
  rElbow: Point
  rHand: Point
  lElbow: Point
  lHand: Point
} {
  const w = 36
  const h = 28
  const headR = w * 0.15
  const headCy = headR + 1
  const shoulderY = headCy + headR * 1.4
  const lerp = (a: number, b: number) => a + (b - a) * armRaise
  const rElbow = { x: lerp(w * 0.8, w * 0.74), y: lerp(shoulderY * 0.75, shoulderY * 0.45) }
  const rHand = { x: lerp(w * 0.74, w * 0.62), y: lerp(h * 0.1, Math.max(0, h * -0.01)) }
  return {
    headR,
    headCy,
    shoulderY,
    lineWidth: w * 0.09,
    rElbow,
    rHand,
    lElbow: { x: w - rElbow.x, y: rElbow.y },
    lHand: { x: w - rHand.x, y: rHand.y },
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `src/ui/Celebration.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'preact/hooks'
import { celebrationFrame, figureGeometry, type CelebrationFrame } from './celebrationMath'
import { Icon } from './Icon'

/** The little figure with hearts on the arrival card in the last 24 hours. Replays when `playKey` changes. */
export function Celebration({ playKey }: { playKey: number }) {
  const [frame, setFrame] = useState<CelebrationFrame | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof matchMedia === 'function' && matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) return
    const start = performance.now()
    let raf = 0
    const tick = (now: number) => {
      const next = celebrationFrame(now - start)
      setFrame(next)
      if (next) raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick)
    }
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick)
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf)
  }, [playKey])

  if (!frame) return null
  const g = figureGeometry(frame.armRaise)
  const arm = (shoulderX: number, elbow: { x: number; y: number }, hand: { x: number; y: number }) =>
    `M${shoulderX} ${g.shoulderY} L${elbow.x} ${elbow.y} L${hand.x} ${hand.y}`
  const line = { stroke: 'url(#celebration-green)', fill: 'none', strokeLinecap: 'round' as const, strokeLinejoin: 'round' as const }

  return (
    <div class="celebration" aria-hidden="true" style={{ opacity: frame.opacity }}>
      <div style={{ transform: `translateY(${frame.offsetY}px) rotate(${frame.rotation}deg) scale(${frame.scale})` }}>
        <svg width="36" height="28" viewBox="0 0 36 28">
          <defs>
            {/* userSpaceOnUse: an objectBoundingBox gradient would vanish on the zero-width torso line. */}
            <linearGradient id="celebration-green" gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="28">
              <stop offset="0" style={{ stopColor: '#5ee07f' }} />
              <stop offset="1" style={{ stopColor: '#2fb34f' }} />
            </linearGradient>
          </defs>
          <circle cx="18" cy={g.headCy} r={g.headR} fill="url(#celebration-green)" />
          <path d={`M18 ${g.shoulderY} L18 28`} style={{ ...line, strokeWidth: g.lineWidth * 1.8 }} />
          <path d={arm(19, g.rElbow, g.rHand)} style={{ ...line, strokeWidth: g.lineWidth }} />
          <path d={arm(17, g.lElbow, g.lHand)} style={{ ...line, strokeWidth: g.lineWidth }} />
        </svg>
      </div>
      {frame.hearts.map((h, i) => (
        <span
          key={i}
          class="celebration-heart"
          style={{ opacity: h.opacity, transform: `translate(calc(-50% + ${h.x}px), calc(-50% + ${h.y}px)) scale(${h.scale})` }}
        >
          <Icon name="Heart" size={h.size} color="var(--red)" />
        </span>
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}
```

Preact core sets SVG attributes exactly as written, so camelCase attribute names such as `strokeWidth` would be ignored by the browser. That's why every stroke and stop color goes through `style`, where camelCase CSS properties work.

```tsx
import { Fragment } from 'preact'
import type { ColorToken } from '../copy/colors'
import { cardCountdown } from '../copy/format'
import type { IconName } from '../icons/iconPaths'
import { Celebration } from './Celebration'
import { cssColor } from './colors'
import { Icon } from './Icon'

export interface CountdownCardProps {
  title: string
  icon: IconName
  color: ColorToken
  target: number
  now: number
  subtitle: string | null
  celebrate?: boolean
  celebrateKey?: number
}

/** "Back Home In" / "Leaves Home In" card (CountdownCardView). */
export function CountdownCard({ title, icon, color, target, now, subtitle, celebrate = false, celebrateKey = 0 }: CountdownCardProps) {
  const css = cssColor(color)
  const parts = cardCountdown(target - now)
  return (
    <section class="card glass countdown">
      <div class="countdown-head">
        <Icon name={icon} size={28} color={css} />
        <h2>{title}</h2>
      </div>
      <div class="countdown-value" role="timer">
        {parts == null ? (
          <span class="num" style={{ color: css }}>Now!</span>
        ) : (
          parts.map((part, i) => (
            <Fragment key={part.unit}>
              {i > 0 && <span class="gap">{'  '}</span>}
              <span class="num" style={{ color: css }}>{part.value}</span>
              <span class="unit">{part.unit}</span>
            </Fragment>
          ))
        )}
      </div>
      {subtitle && <p class="countdown-sub">{subtitle}</p>}
      {celebrate && <Celebration playKey={celebrateKey} />}
    </section>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { Icon } from './Icon'

/** EstimatedReturnCardView's text. */
export function fallbackText(daysRemaining: number): string {
  if (daysRemaining <= 0) return 'Soon'
  return daysRemaining === 1 ? '~1 day' : `~${daysRemaining} days`
}

export function FallbackCard({ daysRemaining }: { daysRemaining: number }) {
  return (
    <section class="card glass countdown">
      <div class="countdown-head">
        <Icon name="House" size={28} color="var(--green)" />
        <h2>Back Home In</h2>
      </div>
      <div class="countdown-value">
        <span class="num" style={{ color: 'var(--green)' }}>{fallbackText(daysRemaining)}</span>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/ui/Footer.tsx`**

```tsx
import { agoText, clockText } from '../copy/format'
import { Icon } from './Icon'
import type { Problem } from './problem'

export interface FooterProps {
  now: number
  lastSuccessAt: number | null
  newestUpdatedAt: number | null
  problem: Problem
  refreshing: boolean
  onRefresh: () => void
  onSignOut: () => void
  signOutLabel: string
  viewerTz: string
  h24: boolean
}

/** SyncStatusBarView freshness colors: under 2 min green, under 5 blue, under 10 orange, otherwise red. */
export function freshnessColor(ageMs: number): string {
  if (ageMs < 120_000) return 'var(--green)'
  if (ageMs < 300_000) return 'var(--blue)'
  if (ageMs < 600_000) return 'var(--orange)'
  return 'var(--red)'
}

/** Freshness rows between sign-out and refresh (spec §4.5). */
export function Footer(p: FooterProps) {
  return (
    <footer class="footer">
      <button type="button" class="glass round" aria-label={p.signOutLabel} title={p.signOutLabel} onClick={p.onSignOut}>
        <Icon name="SignOut" size={12} />
      </button>
      <div class="sync">
        {p.problem ? (
          <p class="sync-row sync-problem">
            <Icon name="Warning" size={12} color="var(--orange)" />
            <span>{problemText(p)}</span>
          </p>
        ) : (
          <div class="sync-rows">
            {p.lastSuccessAt != null && (
              <p class="sync-row">
                <Icon name="Cloud" size={11} color={freshnessColor(p.now - p.lastSuccessAt)} />
                <span>{agoText('Checked', p.now - p.lastSuccessAt)}</span>
              </p>
            )}
            {p.newestUpdatedAt != null && (
              <p class="sync-row">
                {/* Neutral on purpose: a schedule untouched for days is normal (spec §4.5). */}
                <Icon name="ArrowCircleUp" size={11} color="var(--text-2)" />
                <span>{agoText('Schedule updated', p.now - p.newestUpdatedAt)}</span>
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      <button type="button" class="glass round" aria-label="Refresh" title="Refresh" disabled={p.refreshing} onClick={p.onRefresh}>
        <Icon name="ArrowClockwise" size={12} />
      </button>
    </footer>
  )
}

function problemText(p: FooterProps): string {
  if (p.problem?.kind === 'offline') {
    return p.lastSuccessAt != null ? `Offline — showing the schedule from ${clockText(p.lastSuccessAt, p.viewerTz, p.h24)}` : 'Offline'
  }
  return "Couldn't reach CrewLu Cloud — will retry"
}
```

```tsx
import { arrivalWhenText, leaveWhenText, tripDepartsText } from '../copy/format'
import { safeNarrative } from '../copy/narrative'
import { arrivalCard, leaveCard, statusVisual } from '../copy/visuals'
import type { AirportIndex } from '../data/airports'
import type { Snapshot } from '../engine/snapshot'
import { CountdownCard } from './CountdownCard'
import { DaysHomePill } from './DaysHomePill'
import { DemoBanner } from './DemoBanner'
import { FallbackCard } from './FallbackCard'
import { Footer, type FooterProps } from './Footer'
import { NarrativeCard } from './NarrativeCard'
import { Title, titleFor } from './Title'

export interface StatusScreenProps {
  snap: Snapshot
  now: number
  viewerTz: string
  /** The pilot's home time zone, for the card subtitles. */
  homeTz: string
  h24: boolean
  airports: AirportIndex
  demo: boolean
  celebrateKey: number
  footer: FooterProps
}

/** The top of Crewluve's main screen, in its card order (spec §4.4). */
export function StatusScreen(p: StatusScreenProps) {
  const { snap } = p
  const spans = safeNarrative(snap, { now: p.now, viewerTz: p.viewerTz, h24: p.h24, airports: p.airports })
  return (
    <main class="screen">
      <Title text={titleFor(snap.hasName ? snap.name : null)} />
      {p.demo && <DemoBanner />}
      <NarrativeCard spans={spans} visual={statusVisual(snap)} ring={snap.ring} />
      {snap.status.kind === 'home' ? <HomeCards {...p} /> : <AwayCard {...p} />}
      <Footer {...p.footer} />
    </main>
  )
}

function HomeCards({ snap, now, homeTz, h24, airports }: StatusScreenProps) {
  const leave = snap.leave
  const visual = leave ? leaveCard(leave) : null
  const subtitle = !leave
    ? null
    : leave.commuterUnlogged
      ? tripDepartsText(airports.city(leave.airport) ?? '', leave.time, homeTz, h24)
      : leaveWhenText(leave.time, homeTz, h24)
  return (
    <>
      {leave && visual && (
        <CountdownCard title={visual.title} icon={visual.icon} color={visual.color} target={leave.time} now={now} subtitle={subtitle} />
      )}
      {snap.daysHome != null && snap.daysHome >= 1 && <DaysHomePill name={snap.name} days={snap.daysHome} />}
    </>
  )
}

function AwayCard({ snap, now, homeTz, h24, airports, celebrateKey }: StatusScreenProps) {
  const arrival = snap.arrival
  if (!arrival) {
    return snap.fallbackDaysRemaining != null ? <FallbackCard daysRemaining={snap.fallbackDaysRemaining} /> : null
  }
  const visual = arrivalCard(snap, arrival, airports)
  const city = airports.city(arrival.airport)
  const when = arrivalWhenText(arrival.time, homeTz, h24)
  return (
    <CountdownCard
      title={visual.title}
      icon={visual.icon}
      color={visual.color}
      target={arrival.time}
      now={now}
      subtitle={city ? `${when} · ${city}` : when}
      celebrate={snap.celebrate}
      celebrateKey={celebrateKey}
    />
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/ui/hooks/useNow.ts`**

```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from 'preact/hooks'

/** The current time, re-rendering every `intervalMs`. One second, because the countdowns tick each second. */
export function useNow(intervalMs = 1000): number {
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => Date.now())
  useEffect(() => {
    const id = setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), intervalMs)
    return () => clearInterval(id)
  }, [intervalMs])
  return now
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/ui/hooks/useVisibilityCount.ts`**

```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from 'preact/hooks'

/** Counts returns to the page, so the celebration can replay like the app's scenePhase trigger. */
export function useVisibilityCount(): number {
  const [count, setCount] = useState(0)
  useEffect(() => {
    const onChange = () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') setCount((c) => c + 1)
    }
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onChange)
    return () => document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onChange)
  }, [])
  return count
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/ui/hooks/useAirports.ts`**

```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from 'preact/hooks'
import { buildAirportIndex, loadAirports, type AirportIndex } from '../../data/airports'

/**
 * The airport list. If it won't load, an empty index at once, so the screen shows airport codes instead
 * of city names (spec §8). Then one more try after `retryMs`, which swaps in the city names if it works.
 */
export function useAirports(retryMs = 3000): AirportIndex | null {
  const [index, setIndex] = useState<AirportIndex | null>(null)
  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true
    let retry: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined
    const show = (airports: AirportIndex) => {
      if (alive) setIndex(airports)
    }
    loadAirports()
      .then(show)
      .catch(() => {
        show(buildAirportIndex([]))
        if (alive) retry = setTimeout(() => void loadAirports().then(show).catch(() => {}), retryMs)
      })
    return () => {
      alive = false
      clearTimeout(retry)
    }
  }, [retryMs])
  return index
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `src/ui/hooks/useSchedule.ts`**

```ts
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'preact/hooks'
import type { ScheduleCache } from '../../data/cache'
import { ScheduleFetchError } from '../../data/fetchSchedule'
import type { RawSchedule } from '../../data/rows'
import type { Problem } from '../problem'

export interface ScheduleState {
  raw: RawSchedule | null
  lastSuccessAt: number | null
  problem: Problem
  refreshing: boolean
}

export type ScheduleHandle = ScheduleState & { refresh: () => void }

export interface UseScheduleOptions {
  load: () => Promise<RawSchedule>
  cache: ScheduleCache
  intervalMs?: number
  onAuthError?: () => void
  isOnline?: () => boolean
}

export const REFRESH_MS = 5 * 60_000
const RETURN_MIN_GAP_MS = 30_000

/** The cached schedule first, then CrewLu Cloud, refreshed every 5 min while visible and when the visitor returns (spec §5). */
export function useSchedule({ load, cache, intervalMs = REFRESH_MS, onAuthError, isOnline = () => navigator.onLine }: UseScheduleOptions): ScheduleHandle {
  const [state, setState] = useState<ScheduleState>(() => {
    const cached = cache.load()
    return { raw: cached, lastSuccessAt: cached?.fetchedAt ?? null, problem: null, refreshing: true }
  })
  const latest = useRef({ load, cache, onAuthError, isOnline })
  latest.current = { load, cache, onAuthError, isOnline }
  const busy = useRef(false)
  const lastAttempt = useRef(0)

  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
    if (busy.current) return
    busy.current = true
    lastAttempt.current = Date.now()
    setState((s) => ({ ...s, refreshing: true }))
    try {
      const raw = await latest.current.load()
      latest.current.cache.save(raw)
      setState({ raw, lastSuccessAt: raw.fetchedAt, problem: null, refreshing: false })
    } catch (err) {
      setState((s) => ({ ...s, refreshing: false, problem: latest.current.isOnline() ? { kind: 'unreachable' } : { kind: 'offline' } }))
      if (err instanceof ScheduleFetchError && err.isAuth) latest.current.onAuthError?.()
    } finally {
      busy.current = false
    }
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    void refresh()
    const tick = setInterval(() => {
      if (document.visibilityState !== 'hidden') void refresh()
    }, intervalMs)
    const onReturn = () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === 'visible' && Date.now() - lastAttempt.current >= RETURN_MIN_GAP_MS) void refresh()
    }
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onReturn)
    window.addEventListener('online', onReturn)
    return () => {
      clearInterval(tick)
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onReturn)
      window.removeEventListener('online', onReturn)
    }
  }, [refresh, intervalMs])

  return { ...state, refresh: () => void refresh() }
}
```
